# STUDY_OCT10: the whole repository read by thirteen agents on Oct 10, 2026 (13:00-14:45 UTC)

Each of the thirteen areas below was read file by file by one agent (scripts in full, results in full or by head/tail/totals) and checked against
`docs/HANDOFF.md`. A fourteenth agent (the completeness critic) re-verified the discrepancies of the first four areas against the files; the
others' discrepancies are the readers' own claims. The corrections that mattered were folded into `docs/HANDOFF.md` section 16.9. Sections:
the summary, the numbers to remember (each with its source file), the discrepancies with the handoff, scripts that do not run as copied,
open questions. The file list each reader produced is in `research/session_oct9_10/STUDY_OCT10_files.md`.

## research-root

The research root (research/REPORT_24.54.md, research/*.py, research/data/) is the Oct 8-and-earlier research that the follow strategy came from, plus two Oct 8 read-backs (live_trades.py, new_scheme.py) written the same day as releases 0.3 and 0.5. It is a fragment of a larger research series from the owner's older project (README line 5 calls it "the fomo-memebot research"; the files cite reports 21.3, 24.20, 24.45, 24.50 and paths src/analysis/... and data/derived/edge_check/Q/... that do not exist in this repository; the existing trade it keeps referring to is "the Pons seat", a different launchpad with a 99%/6.18%/0.19% per-second launch tax). Report 24.54 answers report 24.45's pending test from the chain instead of with live money: over Sep 29 21:00 - Oct 8 10:30 UTC (blocks 75962099-83244647) operator A (launcher 0x3194e326) made 602 launches, 98-99% of whose swaps are its own wallets (wash 71%, fresh 27%, dust 1%, out 1%); its crowd's first buy lands at block +22/44/64 (p10/50/90) with 34 buys and 1.49 ETH in 30 s, and the deployer pulls the liquidity within 20 minutes on 94% (p50 block 2,393), 563 of 564 pulls sent by the launch's own deployer. The first seat is a trap: 141 of 602 launches drew a real outside buy (>= 0.0002 ETH from an established wallet), 8 before the crowd, and the one clean first-seat case (Sep 30, 1.35% fee, 0.0010 ETH at +1) got no crowd and a 100% pull at +15; the pull answers outside money (2-5% tiers: a $2.6-5 buy pulled within 1 s 65%, within 3 s 74%, n 23; a $5-13 buy 88%/94%, n 16; sub-1% tier: 0%/44% n 16 and 10%/100% n 10, reflex 10-19 blocks, mode 15; first outsider buy -> pull within 5 s on 71% of 141 launches vs a 1.6% placebo), and the 183 outsider positions on the launcher lost -89.1% mean, 164 of them everything, net -3.676 ETH on 4.406 ETH in. The paper seat (+22.1% at 10 s, +54.9% at 20 s, +81.9% at 30 s, 83-90% winning) nets at most -13% to -91% once the reflex is priced. The one variant left, which became follow: on the sub-1% tier (from Oct 5 13:00; 54/133/111/13 launches Oct 5-8), buy 2 blocks after the first real-size buy (the operator's crowd on 306 of 312) and sell 4 blocks later: +3.6% mean, 83% win, p5 -0.8% on 310 launches, identical at $3 and $25 (+3.4%/84%), positive every day (+2.4% to +6.2%); 9 blocks later +6.5%/94%; 553 of the 560 non-dust buys inside the hold are the operator's own; the same rule is about zero (-0.2% to +0.3%) in the 2-5% tiers. live_trades_oct8.txt reads the eight Oct 8 trades of wallet 0xab5a34c3 from the chain: 7 relay trades 13:15-13:20 (+7.5, -100, -100, +19.1, +7.9, -0.5, -100%) and one router trade 14:32 (-9.3%, sold 90% of its tokens); mean -34.4%; every -100% is a sell that landed one block after the pull; the pull came 5-13 blocks after each of our buys (6-15 blocks after the crowd's first buy, 1-2 s) where the operator's other launches that afternoon were pulled 47-9,786 blocks after their first buy; the release-0.3 exit (take-profit on the first paying buy behind ours, deadline landing by buy+4) prices the same eight at +4.0% mean, 6 positive, worst -0.8% in the slow-landing case. new_scheme_oct8.txt (blocks 83394000-83462500, Oct 8 ~14:45-16:42): 455 no-hook ETH pools, 40 by the operator's call 0x2fcb9108 through a fresh launcher copy each; the crowd's burst lands at +9 on 28, +8 on 7 (+7/+10/+11/+13 one or two each); follow prices -0.49% on 0 of 40, the seat at launch+5 or +7 sold on the burst +9.0% on 40 of 40 at $3.08 (+5.8% at $0.39, +8.3% at $1.28, +9.3% at $12.85, worst +6.6%); and a family "0x58daec31 0xac9650d8" (POSM multicall) of 94 pools in two hours priced -1.36% (2 of 94) for follow: this is the volume bot's launch route, visible here on Oct 8 before anyone recognised it (the front strategy was found from the Oct 9 census, handoff 6.5-6.6). SURVEY.md and chain_ordering.txt establish the chain conditions: Robinhood Chain is FCFS (50% of 1,951 transactions offer a tip, 48% of adjacent pairs in descending tip order, 0 of 41 receipts charged above base fee, measured Oct 8 from block 83271939) with a public per-block feed; Arbitrum One switched to priority-fee ordering (Sep 10: 48% descending, 0 of 43 charged; Oct 7: 79%, 32 of 50), consistent with the reported Sep 24 switch; every other chain with a time-keyed launch tax orders by priority fee; two other Robinhood launchpads (Clanker v4, 13 launches in 7 days; Bankr/LONG on Doppler, 1,135 launches a day, only 19 of them paired with ETH, 4% with any swap in the first minute) have the tax but no crowd. The two data files slowrug.json.gz (9.58 MB, 27.3 MB uncompressed: 621 pools of the 1.5-5% fee / tick-200 / no-hook band, 60,174 swaps, 3,542 liquidity events, 61,965 transactions, 231 of the pools operator A's) and slowrug_op.json.gz (8.92 MB, 26.7 MB: 371 operator A pools outside the band, 60,496 swaps, 733 liquidity events, 61,228 transactions) are produced by v4_slowrug_pull.py and read by v4_slowrug_test.py; I re-ran the test script offline on scratchpad copies and its output is byte-identical to research/data/slowrug_test.txt (it needs no RPC because both files carry launch_ts for every pool). All five RPC scripts run as copied in file-path terms (live_trades.py and new_scheme.py find src/v4math.py relative to their own location; eth_utils is installed here) but every one except v4_slowrug_test.py needs the public RPC, and the docstrings/REPORT/SURVEY cite the old repository's paths. Everything the handoff section 5 claims about these files checks out except its Oct 8 net of "-$5.19" (the file's eight returns at a $3.08 stake sum to about -$8.5, which is what the handoff's own section 0 implies: -$4.76 total minus +$3.84 for Oct 9-10 = -$8.60), the "4 wins" wording (the file counts 3 positive; trade 6 is -0.5%), and a 40-vs-42 launch count where new_scheme.py's docstring and the README (42 launches, +8.9%, 30 at +9) describe a later run than the committed output (40, +9.0%, 28 at +9).

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF section 10.1 says the eight Oct 8 trades netted 'about -$5.19'. research/data/live_trades_oct8.txt gives returns +7.5, -100, -100, +19.1, +7.9, -0.5, -100, -9.3% (mean -34.4%) at a 0.0012 ETH = $3.08 stake, which sums to about -$8.5 before gas. The handoff's own section 0 implies the same: 42 trades -$4.76 total and +$3.84 over Oct 9-10 gives Oct 8 about -$8.60. The -$5.19 figure is the inconsistent one (unless trade 8's router stake differed, which the file does not show).
- HANDOFF section 4 (timeline, 'Oct 8 13:15-13:20: the first seven live follow trades through the relay: 4 wins, 3 -100%') and research/live_trades.py's docstring ('four wins and three -100% losses') count trade 6 (-0.5%, no crowd buy behind ours) as a win; research/data/live_trades_oct8.txt itself prints '8 trades: 3 positive, 3 at -100%'. Section 0's 'three -100% trades on the first day' and section 10.1's table are consistent with the file.
- HANDOFF section 5 says new_scheme.py found '40 launches by 16:45 ... the seat +9.0% on paper', which matches research/data/new_scheme_oct8.txt (40 launches, 28 bursts at +9 and 7 at +8, seat +9.0% 40/40). But research/new_scheme.py's own docstring says 'the follow trade -0.5% on all 42 operator launches ... the seat +8.9% at $3, 42 of 42', and README.md (release 0.5 section) says '30 of 42 at +9, 7 at +8' alongside '40 launches by 16:45' and '+9.0% ... 40 of 40': the docstring and the README's 42-count come from a later run (to a later head) than the committed output. Handoff section 6.2 cites 42 copy-scheme launches and +8.9% from yday/seat.py, which matches the docstring, not this file.
- HANDOFF section 4 compresses operator A's supply to 'then 22-56 a day' for Sep 27 - Oct 7. research/data/slowrug_test.txt shows 22-56 a day only for Sep 29 (partial) - Oct 4 (22, 53, 34, 56, 49, 36) and 94, 133, 112 a day on Oct 5-7 (54/133/111 of them on the new sub-1% tier). REPORT_24.54.md states both facts; the handoff kept only the first.
- HANDOFF section 2 describes research/REPORT_24.54.md, research/*.py and research/data/ as 'the original research (Oct 8 and before)' without saying that these files belong to a different, earlier project: REPORT_24.54.md and SURVEY.md cite reports 21.3, 24.20, 24.45 and 24.50, the 'Pons seat' as the existing trade, a 'BuyOnce' relay that is 'Pons-only', and script/data paths src/analysis/<script>.py and data/derived/edge_check/Q/<file> that do not exist in this repository (here research/<script>.py and research/data/<file>). None of those reports or that code is in this repository; README line 5 names the source 'the fomo-memebot research'.
- HANDOFF section 1 and 6.5-6.6 present the volume bot's POSM-multicall launch family as found in the Oct 9 census. research/data/new_scheme_oct8.txt already shows the family on Oct 8 14:45-16:42 as '0x58daec31 0xac9650d8 n 94' (94 launches in about two hours, follow trade -1.36%, 2 of 94 positive), and REPORT_24.54.md calls 0x58daec31 'a multicall many launchers share' (it is the Uniswap v4 PositionManager, per handoff section 1) with 805 outsider positions losing 36.9 ETH on 45.2 ETH in on 29 of its 2-5% pools. Not a contradiction, but the handoff does not note that the front family was visible in the Oct 8 data.
- HANDOFF section 5 says 'the one outsider who bought at launch +1 got no pump and a pull 14 blocks later'; research/data/slowrug_test.txt lists that pool (0x72517b85a0, Sep 30, fee 1.35%) with the buy at +1 and the pull at +15 blocks after launch, i.e. 14 blocks after the buy: consistent, but the two numbers are measured from different origins.
- No file the handoff names in this area is missing: REPORT_24.54.md, chain_ordering_check.py, doppler_first_trades.py, live_trades.py, new_scheme.py, v4_slowrug_pull.py, v4_slowrug_test.py, data/SURVEY.md, data/chain_ordering.txt, data/doppler_first_trades.txt, data/live_trades_oct8.txt, data/new_scheme_oct8.txt, data/slowrug_test.txt, data/slowrug.json.gz, data/slowrug_op.json.gz all exist. The side output v4_slowrug_test.py writes (slowrug_rows.json, 763 KB) was never committed.

**Scripts that do not run as copied**

- research/REPORT_24.54.md, research/data/SURVEY.md and the docstrings of v4_slowrug_pull.py, v4_slowrug_test.py, chain_ordering_check.py and doppler_first_trades.py give usage lines with the old repository's paths: 'python3 src/analysis/<script>.py ... data/derived/edge_check/Q/<file>'. In this repository the scripts are research/<script>.py and the data research/data/<file>; the scripts take their paths as arguments, so they run once the arguments are corrected.
- research/v4_slowrug_test.py runs as copied and offline: 'python3 research/v4_slowrug_test.py research/data/slowrug.json.gz research/data/slowrug_op.json.gz' reproduces research/data/slowrug_test.txt byte for byte (verified here from scratchpad copies). It only contacts the RPC for launch timestamps missing from the data, and both files carry launch_ts for every pool (621 and 371). It writes '<first-argument minus .json.gz>_rows.json' beside the first input (763 KB; would appear as an untracked research/data/slowrug_rows.json if run in place). Stdlib only.
- research/v4_slowrug_pull.py: needs the public RPC and eth_utils; a 7.3M-block window with ~62k transaction lookups and ~1,000 pools of logs; the rate-limited RPC (429s) makes it slow and it may give up ('rpc gave up' after 12 tries). It writes uncompressed JSON (27 MB); the repository holds gzipped copies, so gzip the output afterwards. Do not re-run against the RPC per the task rules; the data is in the gz files.
- research/live_trades.py: imports src/v4math.py via sys.path = <script dir>/../src, correct as copied (v4math exports sell_out_int, buy_out_int, active_liquidity). Needs eth_utils (installed here) and the RPC: block_at is a binary search of eth_getBlockByNumber over the whole chain (about 27 calls per bound), then Initialize logs, swap/liquidity logs over the window + 12,000 blocks, and batched transaction lookups. Exact re-run: 'python3 research/live_trades.py 0xab5a34c3f41e57fdee1a1d6f19d3e21f46df3ea8 "2026-10-08 11:30" "2026-10-08 15:00"'.
- research/new_scheme.py: same import mechanism, same RPC needs; the second argument (last block) is optional and defaults to the chain head, so running it now with only '83394000' would scan from Oct 8 to the present (1.5M+ blocks of Initialize logs, hooked and not) and most likely exhaust the RPC; pass 83462500 to reproduce research/data/new_scheme_oct8.txt.
- research/chain_ordering_check.py: standalone (stdlib), needs the RPC it is pointed at; the Arbitrum run needs https://arb1.arbitrum.io/rpc; the 'now' mode measures the last 3,000 blocks at run time, so it cannot reproduce chain_ordering.txt's third line exactly; 300 full blocks plus 40 receipts per run.
- research/doppler_first_trades.py: needs eth_utils and the RPC; the window is 'the last 24 h' relative to the current head, so it cannot reproduce doppler_first_trades.txt exactly; samples 200 pools (seed 3).
- All RPC scripts send User-Agent 'Mozilla/5.0 Chrome/128' to get past the RPC's front; eth_getLogs calls split recursively on 'exceeds limit' errors; batch sizes 50 (transactions/blocks) and 60-90 pools (logs). Explorer robinhoodchain.blockscout.com is unreachable from scripts (handoff section 1), so nothing here uses it.
- No research-root file hardcodes the Oct 9-10 scratch path (/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-.../scratchpad) or /home/user/v2pons; those appear only under research/session_oct9_10/.

**Open questions**

- Where are reports 21.3, 24.20, 24.45 and 24.50, the Pons engine and the BuyOnce relay that REPORT_24.54.md and SURVEY.md build on? They are not in this repository (README calls the source 'the fomo-memebot research'); report 24.45's 'two early buyers' and its +14% to +92% first-seat paper numbers are quoted here but not reproducible from these files.
- Why does HANDOFF 10.1 say the Oct 8 trades netted about -$5.19 when the file's eight returns at $3.08 sum to about -$8.5 (and the handoff's section 0 arithmetic gives -$8.60)? Possibly results.py accounting (the Oct 8 trades predate the engine's trade_done logging in 0.3) or a different stake on the router trade; worth settling before quoting a lifetime P&L.
- new_scheme.py's docstring (42 launches, +8.9%, 30 at +9) describes a run that is not the committed output (40, +9.0%, 28 at +9); the output of that later run was not kept. Does yday/seat.py in research/session_oct9_10 (handoff 6.2, '42 of 42 +8.9%') cover the same 42 launches?
- The volume bot's family was already in the Oct 8 new_scheme scan as '0x58daec31 0xac9650d8' (94 launches in two hours, follow -1.36%) and in report 24.54 as 29 POSM-launched 2-5% pools whose outsiders lost 36.9 ETH on 45.2 ETH in. Are those 29 band pools (fees 2%, 5%, 4.9%, tick 200) the volume bot's other families (handoff 1 lists 9.333/40/2 ETH families with odd fees) or a different operator using the PositionManager? Not resolved by these files.
- live_trades_oct8.txt section 4 shows operator A pulling an untouched 1% launch (11:46:15) only 47 blocks (5 s) after its first buy with 5 crowd buys, and two launches (12:28:39, 12:43:32) with no crowd at all pulled at +4910 and +15994: the operator's own fast-pull mode predates our trades. For the follow protection item (handoff 12.3) it matters how often the operator pulls within a few blocks of its own first buys with nobody else in the pool; the sub-1% reflex figures in slowrug_test.txt (10-19 blocks after an outsider buy) were measured before Oct 8, and on Oct 8 the pull came 5-13 blocks after our buy, faster than that reflex.
- The sub-1% follow backtest's per-day means fell from +6.2% (Oct 5) to +3.2% (Oct 6) to +2.4% (Oct 7) as the operator's crowd sizes changed; the live Oct 9-10 mean (~+2-4%) sits at the low end. Is the edge decaying with the operator's fee/size choices, and does the 9-block hold's +6.5% survive the Oct 9 evening pulls at +5/+6 (it would not: handoff 6.3 says nothing survives a pull 2 blocks after our buy)?
- SURVEY.md's watch items (Robinhood turning on tip ordering via ArbOS 61 getCollectTips; a Virtuals v5 router on Robinhood) have not been re-checked since Oct 8; chain_ordering_check.py 'now' is the one-command check and it is cheap (300 blocks).
- REPORT_24.54.md's outsider counts ('about 2,000 single-use wallets', 'about 50 wash wallets', '0x58daec31: outsiders lost 37 ETH on 45') come from the test script's roles but are not printed as totals in slowrug_test.txt; the _rows.json side file that holds per-pool roles was not committed.
- The relay still holds the worthless tokens of trades 2, 3 and 7 and the wallet 10% of trade 8's (RUNBOOK 4b); nothing to recover, but a future 'sweep' command run should not mistake them for live positions.

## oct9-root: research/session_oct9_10 scratch root (*.py, *.json, *.jsonl, *.txt, README.md) plus the three review helper directories chk/, repro/, rt/

The scratch root of the Oct 9-10 session holds 56 Python scripts, 3 JSON data files (oct8.json 633 KB, oct8_pools.json 990 KB, newlaunch.json 3 KB), 2 JSONL files (replay_seat.jsonl, w.jsonl), one text result (w.out, synthetic) and the 639-line README index; chk/, repro/ and rt/ hold 1, 1 and 3 review-agent harness scripts. Almost everything here is Oct 8 afternoon/evening and Oct 9 probing of operator A done from the public RPC through chain.py, whose API is: constants RPC, H (headers with a Chrome User-Agent), PM (PoolManager 0x8366a39c…, 0x-prefixed), UR and OP (Universal Router 8876…, launcher 3194e326…, both 40-hex WITHOUT 0x), topic hashes T_INIT/T_SWAP/T_ML (keccak of the v4 Initialize/Swap/ModifyLiquidity signatures, 0x-prefixed), W(d,i) = int of 32-byte word i of 0x-hex data, sgn(x) = int256 two's complement, post(body) = raw JSON-RPC POST with 10 retries (4*(i+1) s back-off on HTTP 429, else 2 s; 90 s timeout; RuntimeError("rpc") after 10 failures), rpc(m,p) = one call raising RuntimeError(str(error)[:200]) on a JSON-RPC error, batch(calls) = list of (method, params) sent in JSON-RPC batches of 50, replies sorted by id, returning result-or-None per call (never raises on a per-item failure), logs(flt,a,b) = eth_getLogs with fromBlock/toBlock added, recursively halving the range on a RuntimeError, and block_at(ts) = binary search (lo=1, hi=head) for the first block whose timestamp >= ts; there is no int128 helper, so every script defines s128 locally for Swap amount0/amount1, and `from chain import *` also pulls in json/time/urllib/keccak. The data files pin the Oct 8 picture: oct8_pools.json (written by day.py) is every ETH-paired v4 pool of the Oct 8 UTC day, blocks 82,878,144-83,719,338 (841,195 blocks, 0.10271 s/block): 3,192 pools, 51 through operator A's original launcher ("orig"), 49 copy-scheme launches (call 0x2fcb9108 to a fresh contract, fees 100/200/300 ppm, first in the 14h bucket, 25 in 15h, 17 in 16h, 4 in 17h), 3,092 others (2,093 through the PositionManager 0x58daec31, 195 direct to the PoolManager). oct8.json (written by find.py) is the 15 original-launcher launches from block 83,279,856 (11:28 UTC) to 83,402,530 (14:58) with their 650 Swap/ModifyLiquidity logs; 7 of them carry our relay's swaps and reproduce handoff table 10.1 exactly (buy/sell/pull offsets after launch: 143/149/149 +7.53%, 56/63/62 -100%, 55/62/61 -100%, 48/55/56 +19.08%, 53/60/66 +7.88%, 59/65/66 -0.48%, 109/115/114 -100%; the three -100% sells sold 0 tokens for 0 ETH after the pull); the 8th (router) trade's pool 83386507 has 172 real buys and a pull at +9901. newlaunch.json has 41 [pid, block] rows: row 0 is the original launcher's 14:30 launch (83386507) and rows 1-40 are the first 40 copy-scheme launches (83402323-83462127, 14:58-16:40 UTC). replay_seat.jsonl is the one real result file: the release-0.5 engine in ENTRY=seat dry run on the last 10 copies fires at launch+6 (planned buy +8), sees the burst at +9 (6), +8 (3) or +13 (1), and would take profit on 9 (mean +4.04%, range +1.13% to +8.31%, 3 of them in the same block as the planned buy) and hit the deadline on 1. w.jsonl/w.out are a 7-event synthetic log and the deploy/watch.sh screen it produces (15:45-15:48 Oct 8 timestamps), not trades. Other scripts: the Oct 8 trade forensics (trades.py, blocks.py, exits.py exit-timing table, pred.py pricer check, why.py/why2.py reverted crowd buys of 0.01708 and 0.06835 ETH whose minOut our buy broke, crowdmin.py decoding the crowd's UR calldata at offset 772, all15.py), the copy-scheme study (newlaunch.py, newpaper.py, burst.py, seat.py/seat2.py first-seat pricer, where.py burst signature, reverts.py), the night/evening monitors (overnight.py, overnight_paper.py, evening.py, now.py, now2.py, quiet.py, dry1.py, liq.py, moved.py, newtrades.py, today3/4/5.py), the launch-wallet funding tracers (fund.py-fund4.py), the v2.433 fingerprint scanners fp_d10_30.py/fp_d2_9*.py (blocks 59,391,955-82,879,334 in 100k chunks, outputs not in the repo), pricing probes (bt.py and crowd.py on research/data/slowrug_op.json.gz, tick.py, tp_calc.py, tp_bug*.py), engine harnesses (replay.py of the 8 trades through the engine, replay_seat.py, scen.py) and res_c2.py (a 105-line early results.py). chk/repro.py reproduces engine start with the front halt file present, repro/repro_phantom.py the phantom-profit case (an outsider's 0.011 ETH behind ours makes the bot's 0.12 ETH buy revert), and rt/*.py feed synthetic logs to deploy/results.py. The 44 engine tests pass here (python3 tests/test_engine.py | grep -c ^ok = 44) and the working tree is unmodified. Every script that imports the engine or v4math hardcodes /home/user/v2pons/src, the fp_ scripts hardcode the old scratchpad path, and chain.py is imported bare so scripts must run with cwd = the research directory.

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 6.12 groups `overnight_paper.py, newpaper.py` as 'the Oct 8-9 night: operator A back on its launcher, follow +2.4% on 14 launches, the seat flat'. newpaper.py prices the 40 copy-scheme launches of the Oct 8 afternoon (newlaunch.json rows 1-40, 14:58-16:40 UTC, fees 100-300 ppm) with the follow trade landing first buy +2, plus the crowd's flow — not the night. Only overnight_paper.py is the night (original-launcher launches since block 83,500,000), and its own print label 'since Oct 8 21:30' does not match that block (~17:44 UTC by the day's anchors).
- HANDOFF 6.12 groups `evening.py, exits.py` as 'the Oct 9 evening pulls at +5/+6'. exits.py works on oct8.json: it is the Oct 8 exit-timing table for the 7 relay trades (return if the sell landed at buy+1..+7, X where the pull was first). Only evening.py (our trades in the last 6 h, crowd A from money/pots.json) is the Oct 9 evening piece.
- HANDOFF 6.12 says `fund*.py (who funds the crowd)`. fund.py-fund4.py trace the funders of the launch (deployer) wallets of sampled launches (binary search on historical balance in fund.py/fund2.py, block scans in fund3.py/fund4.py), not the crowd wallets. fund.py/fund2.py need archive state the public RPC does not serve (handoff section 1 says 'no archive state'); fund2.py catches 'state not available'.
- HANDOFF 6.12 says `tp_bug*.py (a take-profit rounding check)`. tp_bug.py/tp_bug2.py inspect one live pool (id prefix 0x12c952fa9d) around our buy block: the txs carrying the token, their value vs the swap's ETH, receipt status — a take-profit trigger probe. The rounding check is tick.py (tick_at vs sqrt_at_tick on 222,000 prices); tp_calc.py is the front take-profit room math.
- HANDOFF 6.12 says `oct8.json, oct8_pools.json (every Oct 8 pool)`. oct8.json holds only the 15 operator-A (original launcher) pools from 11:28 to 14:58 UTC with their logs; oct8_pools.json is the 3,192 ETH-paired pools of the full Oct 8 UTC day. `newlaunch.json (the first 40 copy-scheme launches)` has 41 rows: row 0 is the original launcher's 14:30 launch (block 83386507, the 8th live trade's pool); scripts skip it with [1:].
- HANDOFF 6.12's numbers ('follow +2.4% on 14 launches, the seat flat') have no result file at the root: the only real result is replay_seat.jsonl (10 seat decisions, 9 take-profits mean +4.04%, 1 deadline) and w.out is synthetic. The overnight_paper/newpaper/seat/evening/exits/bt/crowd outputs were not saved.
- HANDOFF 6.2 and 5 say 'Oct 8's 46 original-launcher launches', '42 copy-scheme launches' and '40 launches by 16:45'; oct8_pools.json counts 51 orig and 49 copy launches on Oct 8 UTC (newlaunch.json's 40 copies end at 16:40 UTC). The 46/42 are presumably after eligibility gates (fee < 1%, liquidity >= 1e21, a real buy) but the handoff does not say so.
- HANDOFF 3.1 says the original launcher's first real buy comes '40-60 blocks in'; in oct8.json's 13 original-launcher pools with a real buy the offsets are 44-208 (median 67; 6 of 13 above +90).
- HANDOFF 1 lists 0xe71a69f4… among 'the volume bot's decoys (its own wallets, in every pool)'; quiet.py and moved.py call it 'the operator's dust wallet that buys every operator launch at +3/+4' (nonce 91,020 by Oct 8 14:32). The attribution of this chain-wide dust wallet is inconsistent between the handoff and the scripts.
- HANDOFF 6.12 does not mention res_c2.py (an early 105-line results.py snapshot), scen.py (the 0.9 review scenarios A/B/C that led to 506a254), bt.py/crowd.py (pricing on research/data/slowrug_op.json.gz), day.py/find.py (the writers of the two JSON files), reverts.py, where.py, moved.py, pred.py, why*.py, trades.py, blocks.py, all15.py, w.jsonl/w.out, or the today3/4/5 variants.
- research/session_oct9_10/README.md (the index the handoff points to in section 2) lists a `tests/` directory among the review-agent directories; no such directory exists under research/session_oct9_10.
- replay.py's docstring says it replays 'through the release 0.3 engine' and sets ROUTE=router; the current engine (0.9) has no ROUTE setting, so run today it replays through 0.9's follow logic. The event fields it reads (trig_seq, plan_buy_seq, after_buy, why, tp_ret) still exist.

- sys.path.insert(0, '/home/user/v2pons/src') is hardcoded in bt.py, burst.py, crowdmin.py, dry1.py, exits.py, fp_d10_30.py, fp_d2_9.py, fp_d2_9_all.py, fp_d2_9_v2.py, liq.py, newpaper.py, newtrades.py, overnight_paper.py, pred.py, replay.py, replay_seat.py, seat.py, tick.py, today3/4/5.py, tp_calc.py, why2.py; scen.py execs /home/user/v2pons/tests/test_engine.py; chk/repro.py, repro/repro_phantom.py and rt/rt.py, rt2.py, run_results.py hardcode /home/user/v2pons/src or /home/user/v2pons/deploy/results.py; tick.py and tp_calc.py open /home/user/v2pons/tests/data/front_launch.json. All break on any other checkout path.
- fp_d10_30.py, fp_d2_9.py, fp_d2_9_all.py, fp_d2_9_v2.py hardcode /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad (SD = …/fp); their chunk caches fp/d10_30_cache, fp/d2_9_cache, fp/d2_9_v2_cache and their outputs fp/d10_30.jsonl, fp/d2_9.jsonl, fp/d2_9_rest_tx.json are not in the repo (fp/ has only the meta JSON and .log files), so fp_d2_9_all.py and fp_d2_9_an.py <jsonl> have no input, and the scanners would refetch ~23.5M blocks of Initialize logs from the rate-limited RPC.

- fund.py and fund2.py need historical eth_getBalance (archive state); the public RPC refuses it — fund.py raises RuntimeError, fund2.py prints 'state not available' and stops. why.py's debug_traceTransaction gets an error reply (no trace API).
- Head-relative monitors (evening.py 6 h, liq.py 2 h, newtrades.py/where.py/overnight*.py since fixed Oct 8 blocks to head, now.py 3 h, now2.py 2 h, quiet.py, reverts.py 3.2 h, today3/4/5.py 25-40 min, tp_bug*.py 40-45 min looking for pool prefix 0x12c952fa9d, fund2/3.py 1.5 h, moved.py, dry1.py, seat.py/seat2.py since 83,394,000) now return different or empty results; the ones scanning from Oct 8 blocks to the head would pull weeks of logs/txs.
- replay.py and replay_seat.py fetch 175 (x8) and 16 (x10) full blocks with transactions via batch(); replay.py sets the obsolete ROUTE env; replay_seat.py overwrites research/session_oct9_10/replay_seat.jsonl (the saved result) when run in place; both start the engine's threads and sleep-poll state.
- rt/rt.py and rt/rt2.py write engine.jsonl, engine.env, HALTED (engine2.jsonl, engine2.env, HALTED2) into the rt/ directory itself (untracked files in the repo; *.env is gitignored, research/**/*.jsonl is not); rt.py takes argv[1] in {a,b,c}; rt2.py's front halt depends on env FH; run_results.py takes a log path as argv[1].
- chk/repro.py: argv[1] = FRONT (0/1), argv[2] present = front already halted; uses tempfile.mktemp; monkeypatches E.rpc and E.Sender — signatures still match the current engine. repro/repro_phantom.py: argv[1] = FRONT_DRY_RUN; needs E.SENDER/E.SIGNER/state['nonce']/state['base_fee'] (present); sleeps 1.3 s before reading the log.
- scen.py runs the entire 44-test suite first (exec of the test file up to its last os.remove(LOG)) and then relies on the test module's globals (reset, front_launch, do_launch, SENT, LOGS, RECEIPTS, swap_receipt, done_at, wait_for, FX, FPID, CROWD, UR, TRIG, BUY_DATA, OUTSIDER, POSM, events, frame, signed, w) and E._u/E._dyn — all present today, but it breaks the moment the test file is refactored.
- seat2.py execs seat.py's source split at '# 1. the first seat' (needs cwd and the same RPC scan); today4.py/today5.py are copies of today3.py differing only in the time window and cut-off.

**Open questions**

- replay_seat.jsonl shows 3 of 10 seat take-profits with after_buy 0, i.e. the burst in the same block (+8) as the planned buy: does the dry run assume our buy is ordered before the burst within that block (FCFS), and is that why the seat sold flat (-0.18%, -0.32%) on its two live trades?
- Where are the outputs of overnight_paper.py ('follow +2.4% on 14 launches'), newpaper.py, seat.py/seat2.py, exits.py, bt.py and crowd.py? Nothing at the root records them, so the 6.12 numbers cannot be re-checked without re-running against the RPC.
- What was the take-profit bug that tp_bug.py/tp_bug2.py investigated on pool 0x12c952fa9d (a feed-seen buy behind ours whose tx value differed from the swap, or a reverted buy)? Did it lead to a code change (the 0.3/0.6 take-profit pricing from the feed) and is the pool's trade in the Oct 9 table?
- Who owns the dust wallet 0xe71a69f434a719cd5afa411568b69716aab3b84a (nonce 91,020 by Oct 8 14:32, buying 2e-6 ETH at +3/+4 in operator A's pools and in the volume bot's)? The handoff files it under the volume bot's decoys, the scripts under operator A; it may be an independent chain-wide dust bot.
- Why do oct8_pools.json's 51 orig / 49 copy launches on Oct 8 become 46 / 42 in handoff 6.2 (eligibility gates? hooked pools? pools with no real buy?) — the exclusion rule should be stated with the paper results.
- Is res_c2.py a committed version of deploy/results.py (which commit) or an unmerged candidate? It is not referenced anywhere.
- fp_d10_30.jsonl / d2_9.jsonl (the root fp_ scripts' outputs) are absent although the README says every result under 1.5 MB was copied — were they over the limit, or are their counts only in fp/d10_30_meta.json / d2_9_meta.json (another area's files)?
- The Oct 8 original-launcher pools in oct8.json show first real buys as late as +142 and +208 while the engine's TRIGGER_MAX_BLOCKS is 150: how many eligible launches miss the trigger window, and is that part of 'follow is not moving' (12.4)?
- why.py's trace of the reverted crowd buys could not run (no trace API); why2.py infers they failed their minOut because of our 0.0012 ETH buy — was the crowd's slippage allowance (minOut/got ratio) ever tabulated, since it bounds how much we can buy ahead of the crowd without breaking its buys (and therefore the operator's tolerance)?

## money-origins (research/session_oct9_10/money and research/session_oct9_10/origins): operator A's money loop, the pots, their origins and exits

This area holds the Oct 9 scans of plain-ETH transfers around operator A's launches (money/, 29 files) and the Oct 9-10 deep dive into where the pots came from and where the profit goes (origins/, 129 top-level files plus base/ 3, eras/ 43, relay/ 4, relayB/ 6, verify/ 25 = 210 files; no script writes to the chain). The loop itself is confirmed exactly as the handoff describes it: after each pull a fresh sweep wallet receives the deployer's payout plus 32 crowd balances (33 inputs) and forwards everything at nonce 0 to a fresh dispatcher, whose nonce-0 transfer funds the next launch wallet and whose nonces 1-32 refill the crowd. On Oct 9 09:00-11:40 UTC (money/pots.json, loop.json, launches_now.json) pot A ran 33 launches with dispatchers receiving 6.9998-7.0153 ETH (mean 7.0095), launch wallets funded 1.76-3.03 ETH, 32 crowd wallets; pot B ran 4 launches (3+1 chains) with dispatchers at 4.8552-4.8573 ETH, 31 crowd wallets, and B's launches were the lowest-fee ones (fees 170/220/912 vs A's 766-4927). loopA_money.json reconstructs pot A hour by hour from its birth: a 7.0394 ETH sweep of 37 single-use buyers plus launch wallet 0xf339ced1 (4.892) at Oct 6 17:37:15 (block 81811984) into 0xe000a8a9, 1.815 ETH of it to 0xcbe18aee then to 0x4268b546, which launched 1.764 ETH at 81821357, was pulled at 81823225, collected 7.0442 and sent it via 0x30837a8f to dispatcher 0xb3db60c7 at 18:14:11-13 (block 81833714-81833736), which created the persistent crowd (30 wallets still in use + 19 extras drained Oct 7 13:27); A's crowd wallets' first token activity is Oct 6 18:14 (firstseen.log, blocks 81834016-81834227), B's is Sep 17 13:48 (65415799-65421363, four nonce-0 funders behind 0xd6d0a709) to Sep 19. The pot stayed 7.04-7.11 ETH through Oct 6-8 (overnight the launch wallet holds 5.76-5.90), jumped from 7.1092 to 8.6585 ETH on Oct 8 11:19-11:48 over 11 launches on the bonding-curve factory 0xe33e9e47 (analysis_e33.txt: sweeps 7.1098 -> 8.6585, launch wallets paying 3.1005-3.4005 ETH each), then 1.6 ETH left the loop at 12:10:05 (0xb63b68dc -> hub 0x3ee51814), and the pot was back at 7.0388 by 21:57, 7.0153 after the 15 overnight pairs (analysis_night.txt: 22:02:43 to 00:29:00, 7.0381 -> 7.0153 ETH), 6.9999 after the 33rd Oct 9 sweep and 6.9994 at rest at 15:08 (0xfff26739 holds 5.3905 + crowd 1.6089, idle since 14:15). The exits are bridged: 0x3ee51814 sent 0.8, 1.0 and 0.25 ETH to Relay deposit addresses (receiver 0x4cd00e38, selector 0x49290c1c, solver 0x6ff95003, rate 0.9955) and 0.8 into a third crowd's sweep 0xdef59ef1 (31 wallets with nonces 156-2209, swept 13:50:03), which paid 1.25 back out through Relay; on Base every recipient (A side 0xd2fc9f25 0.799, 0x7e9e0e2b 0.997, 0xf6a6f8fa 0.249, 0x72fadacc 1.248; B side 0x761be1b5 0.2995, 0x64880aaf 0.3993, 0xe845b142 0.4992, all from B's sweep 0xe7a1c2fb nonces 5-8) forwards at nonce 0 to 0x487cab40, a sweeper (nonce ~7.9k) that batches into hot wallet 0x3304e22d (nonce 6,597,154, 29,656 ETH) every 7-10 minutes and whose other depositors are ordinary Relay bridgers (bsrc1.out: 9 of 13 funded by Relay's Base solver 0xf70da978), i.e. an exchange deposit flow, not a personal wallet. fam.out (cached plain transfers, iterative family labelling: A 74 dispatchers/73 sweeps/72 payers, B 46/48/72) finds 0 plain transfers between the A and B families and 0 shared on-chain counterparties; B's pot path (b_potpath.txt) runs 5.728 ETH on Oct 7 18:08, grows to 6.19 on Oct 8 via its own bonding-curve launches 11:28-11:55 and the 14:59-16:36 copy scheme through collector 0x3fc5b8b8 (payers 3.2-5.046 ETH, three fresh crowd wallets per launch at 0.1/0.1/0.15), and is 4.874 by Oct 9 05:55 after about 1.3 ETH left through 0xe7a1c2fb. The eras work (origins/eras/) clusters all 7,485 launcher launches by shared repeat buyers: 75,447 distinct buyers, 755 in 3+ launches, 14 components covering 1,060 launches (A = L006: 245 launches Sep 11 - Oct 9, 49 crowd wallets of which 30 are today's A crowd; B = L010: 170 launches Sep 18 - Oct 9, 29 crowd, 12 in today's B crowd; L012: 410 launches Sep 21 - Oct 4 with 49 crowd; L009 69, L011 63, L004 58), 6,425 single-shot launches whose 85,630 buyer slots are 73,536 single-launch wallets, and 32 traced sample launches whose pots were 7.64 ETH (Jul 26, 1.0 ETH launch), 8.3 (2.0), 16.36 (8.0), 3.3-3.8 (Aug 28-Sep 5 and the Sep 25 - Oct 8 1.97-ETH 'fresh' track = the ~3.5 ETH small pot), 4.7-8.2 for the L-components and 7.04-7.07 for the Oct 4-6 F40/fresh track that became pot A. Mass 'dust' flows mark the genesis: 3,668 single-use wallets each sent ~1.5e-6 ETH to 0x4268b546 on Oct 6 17:56-18:11 (dust_4268_senders.json, 47 earlier launch wallets totalling 88.68 ETH of launches) and 282 old loop wallets dusted 0x41c8a980 where the Oct 7 pot split rejoined. Everything is read from the public RPC through block caches that were not copied, so almost every script needs the two absolute paths edited and several need inputs (money/win.jsonl, ops/launcher_txs.json, ops/launcher_logs.json, eras/p1.json, origins/vt.pkl, the ops/lch/bc and origins/blk caches) that are absent from the repository.

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 6.4 says analysis_night.txt covers 'Oct 8 22:02 - Oct 9 00:22, fifteen sweep/dispatcher pairs of 7.017-7.038 ETH each'; origins/analysis_night.txt shows the 15 pairs run from 22:02:43 to 00:29:00 (last sweep 83736297 at 00:28:57, dispatcher 00:29:00) and the amounts run 7.0153-7.0381 ETH (the last two pairs are 7.0159 and 7.0153).
- HANDOFF 6.4 'Eras: the operator's launch value moved 0.3 -> 0.45 -> 1.97 ETH over the months': origins/eras/p4.out shows the first era (Jul 26 - Aug 7) launched mostly 1.0 ETH (27 of 63 on Jul 27) with 1.5, 2.0, 8.0 and 10.0 ETH launches; 0.3 ETH is one sampled launch in money/eras.log (block 19404821), not an era; the 0.34-0.5 era is Aug 19 - Sep 9 alongside 1.0-1.35 ETH launches, 1.97 is Sep 13-30, and Oct 1-9 is 0.45-4.95.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'No plain transfer between the A and B families and no shared counterparty (origins/fam.out ...)': fam.out confirms 0 cross-family plain transfers and 0 shared counterparties within the cached Robinhood-chain blocks only; the same session's origins/base/bfwd_AB.txt and loopA_money.json show both families' bridged exits converge on the same Base address 0x487cab40 (the handoff does name it, but 'no shared counterparty' should be read as on-chain plain transfers only), and origins/analysis_e33.txt + b_potpath.txt show A and B both launching on the bonding-curve factory 0xe33e9e47 in the same minutes (Oct 8 11:20-11:55), a behavioural overlap not mentioned.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'Loop B: 4.856 ETH ... running the low-fee launches': only partly supported by the files: on Oct 9 B's three launches were at fees 220, 170 and 912 and A's 33 at 766-4927 (money/launches_now.json), but three of A's were also below 1000 (766, 872, 999) and deployers.json's 24 'orig_lowfee' launches are not attributed to B anywhere in money/ or origins/; A's own copy of the pattern (analysis_e33.txt 'crowd 0x8700722d -> 0xcfcf219e 3.5') shows A also funds launch wallets directly from a crowd wallet.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'Pot A: about 7.01 ETH': true for Oct 9 (6.9998-7.0153) but origins/loopA_money.json shows the pot was 7.0695 on Oct 6 09:46, 7.11 on Oct 8 morning, 8.6585 at Oct 8 11:48 after the bonding-curve launches, and 1.6 ETH left it at 12:10; the handoff gives no hint that the pot grows from market income and is skimmed.
- HANDOFF 6.4 names the result files of the money scans as 'loop.json, pots.json, launches_now.json' produced by 'money/tr.py, windows.py, loop.py, signature.py': pots.json's own note says it was built from part*.jsonl + win.jsonl, but no script in money/ writes pots.json, and win.jsonl (which loop.py and signature.py read) was not copied; loop.py and signature.py therefore cannot be re-run as copied.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'A small pot of about 3.5 ETH': supported by origins/eras/samples.json (3.507-3.571 ETH for the Sep 25 - Oct 8 1.97/0.86/0.95-ETH 'fresh' launches with single-use crowds, and 3.5246 for 0xef441005's track) but the handoff cites no file for it; the 3.5 ETH transfer in analysis_e33.txt belongs to A (from crowd wallet 0x8700722d), not to a third pot.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'the operator's pull ... sweep wallet collects the deployer's payout plus the balances of the 32 crowd wallets': in the Oct 6-7 sessions the sweeps had 49-50 inputs (30 crowd + 19 extras + launch wallet, origins/analysis_birth.txt, analysis_d19.txt, eras/q2.txt A-oct06-restart 'ins 50'), and pot B's sweeps have 32 inputs (31 crowd + payer); '32 crowd' is correct only for pot A from Oct 7 15:36 on (30 crowd) and Oct 8 21:57 on (32).
- research/session_oct9_10/README.md says origins has 129 files (true for the top level; with base/, eras/, relay/, relayB/, verify/ it is 210); the orchestrator's task text said 134 and gave the commit as 3c3f696 while the checkout's HEAD is 4dce46e (not a handoff claim).
- HANDOFF 2 (repository table) says 'Large caches ... were not copied; every script that needs them re-fetches from the public RPC': not true for the eras scripts, which need ops/launcher_txs.json and ops/launcher_logs.json (absent, not re-fetched by any script here) and origins/eras/p1.json (absent; p1.py can regenerate it but takes hours), nor for fam.py/potpath.py/h.py which need origins/vt.pkl built by idx.py from an absent cache.

**Scripts that do not run as copied**

- Every Python script in money/ and origins/ (tr.py, deployers.py, olib.py, analyze.py, activity.py, act2.py, fb.py, fill.py, firstseen.py, h.py, hopback.py, idx.py, poolbuyers.py, potpath.py, pullof.py, winscan.py, fam.py, eras/L.py, eras/summarize.py, verify/first_tok.py, verify/scan.py, verify/tx.py) does sys.path.insert or sets D/S to '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad[/...]'; every shell script (jobs.sh, queue*.sh, runq*.sh, eras/runq*.sh, eras/chain3.sh) cd's to that path. All must be edited to research/session_oct9_10 before anything runs.
- money/loop.py and money/signature.py read sorted(glob('part*.jsonl')) + ['win.jsonl']: win.jsonl (windows.py's output) was not copied -> FileNotFoundError.
- money/tr.py caches to money/blocks.pkl (absent; tolerated, starts empty and re-fetches); money/deployers.py imports protect/load_all.py and protect/classify.py (present) which themselves re-fetch; money/eras.py reads ../ops/launcher_txs.json (ABSENT).
- origins/eras/L.py launches() reads ops/launcher_logs.json and ops/launcher_txs.json (both ABSENT): eras/p1.py, p2-p7.py, trace.py, one.py, who.py, fp.py, nonces.py, t1.py, back.py (via B.py -> L.py) all fail at import time as copied.
- origins/eras/p2-p7.py, trace.py, one.py, nonces.py, who.py, t1.py read eras/p1.json (ABSENT; only regenerable by p1.py: 7,485 launches of eth_getLogs, hours at the RPC's 429 rate).
- origins/fam.py, h.py, potpath.py read origins/vt.pkl (ABSENT) and fam.pkl (ABSENT); idx.py would build vt.pkl from ops/lch/bc/*.json (cache dir ABSENT) and money/blocks.pkl (ABSENT).
- origins/fb.py, fill.py (imports ops/lch/bc.py, present), winscan.py, hopback.py use the ops/lch/bc 250-block cache (ABSENT): they re-fetch everything, at ~45% HTTP 429 (e.g. 31,250 blocks took 15 min with 1,167 requests, queue9.log). origins/olib.py and eras/B.py use origins/blk and eras/blk caches (ABSENT), same effect.
- origins/analyze.py reads state_now.json (present) and the scan_*.jsonl given on the command line; scan_e33.jsonl, scan_night.jsonl, scan_d19c.jsonl, scan_birth.jsonl and any scan_oct8 output were not copied, so analysis_e33/night/d19c cannot be regenerated without re-scanning 35,201 / 7,556 / 4,831 blocks.
- origins/jobs.sh reads jobs.q / jobs.done (absent) and waits on pgrep of other queue processes; queue3-10.sh wait on earlier queue logs and kill processes by name: they are session choreography, not reproducible commands.
- origins/eras/fp.py does exec(open('who.py').read().split('if __name__')[0]) with a relative path (must run from eras/) and writes fp.json (not copied).
- origins/base/*.py, baseact.py, basefwd.py, verify/bsrc.py, verify/btx.py call https://mainnet.base.org (public, rate-limited) and verify/bsrc.py calls https://api.relay.link/requests/v2, which is deprecated (sunset Nov 24, 2026) and already rate-limited (2 of 5 relayB lookups returned 429); v3 requires an x-api-key header (relayB/v3_8dfd.json).
- money/collector.py hard-codes a truncated address '0x8700722d7a' in its A32 set (never matches); harmless but wrong.
- money/windows.py crashed once with RuntimeError('rpc') from chain.post after 10 retries (windows.log) and had to be resumed with the RESUME argument; chain.py's batch() has no partial-failure handling.
- money/probe.out shows robinhoodchain.blockscout.com is behind a Cloudflare challenge: no explorer API from scripts.

**Open questions**

- Who owns the pots and whether A and B are one operator: the files prove no on-chain plain transfer between the families, but both exit through Relay to Base recipients that all forward to the same deposit sweeper 0x487cab40 (an exchange-like address used by unrelated bridgers too), and both ran bonding-curve launches on 0xe33e9e47 in the same minutes on Oct 8; the question is undecidable from the chain alone.
- Where the rest of pot B's overnight shrink went: B was 6.1741 at Oct 8 19:08 and 4.8744 at Oct 9 05:55; found exits from 0xe7a1c2fb are 0.1 (nonce 5), 0.3 (nonce 6), 0.5 (nonce 8) and the 0.2995/0.3993/0.4992 Base arrivals; nonce 7 and the Relay records for 0x8dfda946 and 0x2e727aa6 were rate-limited (relayB/), so ~0.3-0.5 ETH is unaccounted.
- Crowd D (0xdef59ef1, 31 wallets with nonces 156-2209, the oldest persistent crowd seen): which component it is, where its 3.99 ETH went after 0x0c8c381e -> 0x8ef3c8f1, and why A's hub 0x3ee51814 lent it 0.8 and got 1.25 back; this is the strongest hint of a shared operator behind two 'pots'.
- The hub 0x3ee51814: its own funding was never found (no incoming in 83263419-83303419, hops.jsonl) and only nonces 16-19 were traced; what its nonces 0-15 did and whether it links A to earlier loops.
- The pre-birth 7.0695 ETH pot at Oct 6 09:46 (0x795110a4, scan_s6) is 'same-size' but was not linked hop by hop to the 17:37 sweep (loopA_money.json note); and the Oct 7 13:27 split (1.0/3.054/3.005 rejoined at 0x41c8a980, the dust collector of 282 old loop wallets) was not fully traced (analyze_last.json names disp 0x8af8d87b, sweep 0xb82f0373, launch wallet 0x3f977420).
- What the mass dust is: 3,668 fresh wallets each sending 1.5e-6 ETH to 0x4268b546 on Oct 6 17:56-18:11 and 282 old loop wallets dusting 0x41c8a980; it looks like an automated 'drain remaining balance' step over the F40 family's single-use buyers (47 of their funders were launch wallets, 88.68 ETH of launches), which would make the Oct 4-6 fresh-crowd 'F40' track and pot A one operation, but no file states this.
- Why L006 (A) spans Sep 11 to Oct 9 while every current A crowd wallet is first seen Oct 6 18:14: the link is the 19 'extra' wallets (six of them bought on Sep 11 and sit in the 0xd460e53a sweep of 4.666 ETH) that were drained Oct 7 13:27; whether the Sep 11 - Oct 5 gap means the operator ran another family (L012, 410 launches Sep 21 - Oct 4, 49 crowd, 0 overlap with A/B) in between is not resolved.
- The B-genesis chain ends at 0xaa011160 (1.0 ETH, nonce 0, block 65406944, Sep 17 13:35) and two 6.2-ETH fan-in wallets (0xd6d0a709, 0xa2a84b82); where that money came from (an earlier B round, or outside) was not traced.
- Whether pot sizes are chosen: A has been pinned at ~7.0 ETH since Oct 6 (and 7.64 on Jul 26, 8.3 on Jul 27, 16.36 for the 8-ETH launch), B at 5.73 then 4.86, the fresh track at ~3.5; the Oct 8 skim of 1.6 right after growing to 8.66 suggests a target of ~7.0 for A; not stated in the files.
- The relay_deposits_* files (999.72 ETH Oct 8 daytime, 782.74 ETH overnight, 15 solver fills totalling 52.2 ETH) were collected to test whether pot money arrives via Relay; none matches a 7.0 or 4.86 ETH inflow and no conclusion is written down.
- The task text's commit (3c3f696) does not match the checkout (HEAD 4dce46e) and its origins file count (134) does not match the directory (129 top-level, 210 with subdirectories).

## ops-ops2-third: research/session_oct9_10/ops, ops2, third (operator A's launcher pulls/disassembly/permission probe, the A/B crowd and B-launch lists, the Oct 8 copy-scheme collector, the money-loop traces, the A-vs-B mixing tests, the Relay.link/Base cash-out follow, and the third (bonding-curve) launch system)

The three directories hold 288 files (ops 35 + lch 63 + tr 26 + web 3; ops2 66 + bcrowd 13 + verify3 15 + relay 21; third 44 + verify 2), every one of which was read; the counts match the README's. ops/ establishes the A/B crowd split from swap fingerprints (b_list.json: crowd A 32 wallets, crowd B 31, 798 'fresh' single-use wallets, 1 'speck'; 7 B launches Oct 8 05:59 to Oct 9 10:48 with pulls +816 to +18,096 blocks after launch and 0.37-4.47 ETH net crowd inflow each, b_launches.json), pulls the launcher's bytecode (6,984 bytes, hash 42544ef5; the 1,784 per-launch copies differ only in 64 bytes of solc metadata, hash 2793923d) and disassembles it (dis_launcher.txt 4,492 lines, dis_copy.txt 4,505), and shows by eth_call that launch() is permissionless (sim.out/sim_copy.out/lch/sim_out.txt: any random sender with a new salt succeeds on the original and on a copy; an exact replay reverts only because the CREATE2 token already exists). The lch/ money traces (trace.py -> 26 tr/*.json, loop.py/loopback.py -> loop_*.jsonl) reproduce the loop: launch wallet funded 1.0-5.0 ETH at nonce 0 by a fresh dispatcher, dispatcher funded ~7.01-7.11 ETH (A) or ~4.85-6.19 ETH (B) by a fresh sweep that collects the previous deployer's payout plus 30-33 (A) or 20-32 (B) crowd wallets; A's pot grew 7.109 -> 8.658 ETH over 11:19-11:48 Oct 8 and was 7.000-7.015 on Oct 9; B's was 5.74-6.19 on Oct 8 and 4.853-4.874 on Oct 9. The Oct 8 copy scheme ran off collector 0x3fc5b8b8: seeded 3.5 ETH by B dispatcher 0x58fc6f17 (nonce 0, 14:49:29) plus 28 x ~0.09-0.10 ETH from crowd B at 14:50:19, it funded each copy launch wallet (4.76/3.34/3.63/3.48/4.6 ETH) and three 0.1/0.1/0.15 burst buyers, took the payouts back, received 1,433 dust transfers (6.0238 ETH incl. one 5.23 payout) from 374 wallets by 17:24, and exited 6.1803 ETH at nonce 789 (17:33:20) into B's next dispatcher 0xe50dd00a (29 B wallets + 2.1 ETH launch wallet); its latest nonce is 791 (latest.json), and 1,408 of the 1,413 one-shot wallets never sent again. ops2's cache-wide mixing tests found zero plain-ETH transfers and zero shared counterparties between the A side and the B/copy side over 435,750 blocks (mix_pass1.out) and 611,681 blocks (verify3/cachescan.json: 238 hits, none A<->B), and lists.json shows 59 bonding-curve tokens whose listing events name either 13 x A crowd (30-31 wallets each) or 46 x B crowd (4-31), never both. verify3 re-fetched 32 cited money links by hash (29 match; the 3 'BAD' are tolerance/typo artifacts, all 32 succeeded on chain), followed the cash-outs: 8 Relay.link bridge deposits (contract 0x4cd00e38, selector 0x49290c1c, chain 4663 -> Base 8453, rate 0.99878) on Oct 8 (A side via 0x3ee51814: 0.8/1.0/0.25 ETH; crowd-D sweep 1.25; B side via 0xe7a1c2fb: 0.1/0.3/0.4/0.5 at 19:24-19:31) all forwarded on Base to 0x487cab40 (nonce 7,985, exchange-like), while Relay saw 5,538 deposits (842.98 ETH) Oct 8-9 in total. The third system is executor 0xbbd947c3 (deployed block 68,791,584 by relayer 0x37bd8ddc; 2,007 dump events from block 68,796,696 = Sep 21 12:28 UTC) dumping tokens created on factory 0x7ed598bc via router 0xe33e9e47 (684,002 factory creates since block ~27.0M; 5,744 in the last 2M blocks by 4,598 creators); on Oct 9 955 launches by 825 creators (620 EOA, 281 EIP-7702 delegated, 54 contracts; only 9 launches by delegates embedding the executor), and in the 11:21-12:12 window 92 launches of which 9 were dispatcher-funded bursts (9-16 wallets, 0.50-0.77 ETH) and 9 dumped (0-1.455 ETH each, 1.729 ETH to 9 collectors). The most important thing the files show that the handoff does not: loop B itself moved onto that bonding-curve platform on Oct 9: its 10:48 launch payout (4.806 -> 4.8535 -> 4.8534 ETH) reached dispatcher 0x6169a10f at 11:48:45, which sent 3.2 ETH to 0xba8e56d0 (the 11:51:30 third-system launch, 13-wallet 4.30 ETH burst) and 0.083-0.10 ETH to 18 crowd-B wallets (third/t6.json, verify/v1.json 'loopB'), and the last four curves in lists.json (blocks 84,134,665-84,174,827) each list 18 B wallets; crosscheck.json reports 0 third<->A/B hits only because the fresh dispatcher is in neither address set. Both A and B launch wallets also call the bonding-curve router (0xe33e9e47 0xf85f8e41) right after every v4 launch (loop_A/B2 'calls'). Nothing here was run against the RPC; every script carries the dead scratchpad path and most need caches (ops/lch/bc, launcher_txs.json, launcher_logs.json, other_launcher_logs.json, bcurve_factory.json, population.json, money/blocks.pkl) that were not copied into the repository.

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 6.4 and the 1 table say the 1,784 copies were 'each created by its launch wallet at nonce 0' / 'each CREATE(recipient, 0)'; ops2/copies_create.out: 1,782 at nonce 0 and 2 at nonce 1. Also the 1 table ties the copies to 'the Oct 8 14:59 scheme', but ops/lch/alllaunchers2.log shows copy emitters from block ~68.4M (August) onward (e.g. 151 of 152 Launched events in 71.17M-71.42M were from copies); the copies-per-launch pattern predates Oct 8 by two months.
- HANDOFF 6.4 describes ops/ and ops2/ as holding 'the full launcher transaction and log pulls (b_launches.json, lists.json)'. b_launches.json is the swap aggregation of the 7 B launches and lists.json is the 59 bonding-curve listing events naming A/B wallets. The actual full pulls (ops/launcher_logs.json, ops/launcher_txs.json, ops/lch/other_launcher_logs.json, ops/bcurve_factory.json, ops/lch/population.json) are not in the repository; the README's 'every result file under 1.5 MB' rule dropped them.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'funded 0.15 ETH through two hops from 0xcb88b74c': ops2/creation.out shows three transfers (0xcb88b74c 6.0586 -> 0x5ec3c428 0.15 -> 0x3130ea49 0.14999 -> 0x3b11cafa), i.e. two intermediate wallets; ops/lch/creator_hops2.txt traces six further hops back to 0x254535ac (Jul 25 21:59). Wording only.
- HANDOFF 6.4 (and README) call third/ 'a bonding-curve factory with EIP-7702 delegated creator wallets; first launch Sep 21 12:18 UTC, block 68,790,933'. third/fac_logs.log: the factory 0x7ed598bc has 684,002 create events back to block ~27.03M; Sep 21 is only the first launch dumped by executor 0xbbd947c3 (deployed block 68,791,584, third/verify/v1.json). third/creators9.json: of 955 Oct 9 launches only 281 (29%) have 7702-delegated creators, 620 are plain EOAs, and only 9 launches come from delegates that embed the executor; classify_84116000_84147499.json: 58 of 92 creators are EOAs.
- HANDOFF 6.4 says the pots are separate ('No plain transfer between the A and B families ... at least 2, most likely 3 separate pots') and 6.4/12.6 treat the bonding-curve system as a parked, unrelated third system. The files show loop B itself launched on that platform: third/t6.json + ops/lch/tr/0x2a928e99.json + third/verify/v1.json 'loopB' trace B's 10:48 Oct 9 payout (4.806 -> 4.8535 -> 4.8534 ETH) into dispatcher 0x6169a10f, which sent 3.2 ETH to 0xba8e56d0 (the 11:51:30 bonding-curve launch) and 0.083-0.10 ETH to 18 crowd-B wallets; ops/lists.json shows the last 4 curves (>= 84,134,665) each listing 18 B wallets with 0xba8e56d0 as a creator; and loop_A.jsonl/loop_B2.jsonl show every A and B launch wallet calling 0xe33e9e47 0xf85f8e41 (a bonding-curve launch) after each v4 launch, with lists.json tying 13 curves to A's launch wallets and 46 to B's. third/crosscheck.json's 0 hits is an artifact (the fresh dispatcher is in neither third_set.json nor pots_addrs.json) and classify.py's src_in_pots=0 is structurally blind for the same reason.
- HANDOFF 6.4 says 'A small pot of about 3.5 ETH' and names only pots A, B and the small one; ops2/g_def5.txt, g_0c8c.txt and verify3/links.out X1/X2 document a fourth crowd ('D', 32 wallets, ~5.3 ETH swept to 0xdef59ef1 at 13:50 Oct 8) that exchanged money with the A side's 0x3ee51814 (0.8 ETH in, 1.25 ETH out), itself funded 1.6 ETH by A's launch wallet 0xb63b68dc, so A and D are linked by plain transfers. ops2/mix4.py (the four-pot test) has no saved output.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'A shared Base-chain address 0x487cab40 is an exchange-like collector' omits how the money got to Base: verify3/relay/*.json and ops2/dep4cd.json show Relay.link bridge deposits (contract 0x4cd00e38, selector 0x49290c1c) from 0x6564816d (0.8 ETH, 12:11:06Z), 0xab7650b2 (1.25), 0x639266c3 / 0x73c1a347 / 0x395a6a62 / 0x8dfda946 (0.1/0.3/0.4/0.5) on Oct 8, every one landing at 0x487cab40 on Base.
- HANDOFF 6.4 'Pot A: about 7.01 ETH' is the Oct 9 value; ops/lch/loop_A.jsonl shows the A pot growing from 7.109 to 8.658 ETH between 11:19 and 11:48 Oct 8 (the pot is not constant) and loop_B2.jsonl shows B at 5.74-6.14 ETH on Oct 8 vs 4.853-4.874 on Oct 9.
- README index (not the handoff) says ops2/cross.py writes cross.json; ops2/cross.json does not exist (only ops2/bcrowd/cross.json). ops2/gapscan.jsonl exists but is empty (the run found 0 hits), ops/bprof.out, ops/lch/inj_A0945.txt, ops/lch/log_C2.txt, ops/lch/loop_B.jsonl and ops/lch/web/c79587b4.out are 0-byte files, and ops/oct9b.out is a traceback (oct9b.json was never produced).
- verify3/links.out reports '29 of 32 match'; the three BAD rows are tolerance/typo artifacts in the script's expected values (A1 3.242719 vs 3.2427, A2 7.015085 vs 7.0151, R2 nonce 1418 vs 1417) and all 32 txs succeeded with the stated from/to, so every money link cited in 6.4 is confirmed, not refuted.

**Scripts that do not run as copied**

- Every script in ops/, ops/lch/, ops2/, ops2/bcrowd/, ops2/verify3/ and third/ does sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad[/ops|/ops/lch|/ops2|/protect]') and/or sets D/S/O/M to that path; the directory is now research/session_oct9_10 and must be rewritten before any of them runs.
- ops/lch/bc.py cache directory ops/lch/bc/ (250-block q<c>.json files) was not copied; bc.py/lib2.py re-fetch missing chunks from the public RPC (slow, 429-heavy), but the cache-only scripts (ops2/agap.py, anonce.py, copyset.py, cross.py, dep4cd.py, fresh.py, fwd2.py, grepc.py, hashes.py, latest.py, mix.py, mix4.py, potnonce.py, potnonce9.py, q1-q5.py, who.py, bcrowd/bpothist.py, bcrowd/bw.py, verify3/cachescan.py, verify3/attrib.py, third/crosscheck.py, ops/lch/grepc.py) would silently return empty results with no cache.
- ops/launcher_logs.json and ops/launcher_txs.json (the 7,485-launch pulls) are missing: needed by ops/lchtx.py, ops/lch/population.py, ops/lch/decode.py (__main__), ops2/pop.py, ops2/receipts.py, ops2/sim.py, ops2/verify3/attrib.py; ops/lchlogs.py recreates them with a full-history eth_getLogs scan.
- ops/lch/other_launcher_logs.json (1,784 copy emitters) missing: needed by ops2/copies_create.py and ops2/creation.py (alllaunchers2.py rebuilds it, 65 x 250k-block log scans).
- ops/bcurve_factory.json missing: needed by ops/bcurve2.py and ops/lists.py (ops/bcurve.py rebuilds it). ops/bcurve2.py is an unfinished fragment.
- ops/lch/population.json missing: needed by ops/lch/depfund.py and ops/lch/timeline.py (population.py rebuilds it from the missing launcher pulls).
- money/blocks.pkl missing (ops2/cross.py, verify3/cachescan.py, ops2/potnonce9.py read it; cross/cachescan catch the exception, potnonce9 would crash). money/loop.json, money/pots.json, money/launches_now.json and money/part*.jsonl do exist.
- ops/oct9b.py crashes with NameError: 'D' (imports bcx and sets, neither defines D) as ops/oct9b.out records; ops/sets.py runs its file loads at import time.
- ops/b_list.py, ops/b_launches.py, ops/fpcmp.py, ops/lch/launches.py import protect/load_all.py and protect/classify.py, which exist but need the protect/ pool caches (not copied); b_list.py/b_launches.py write to the current directory.
- ops2/fwd.py, ops2/gapscan.py, bcrowd/bpothist.py read '../ops/b_list.json' / '../../ops/b_list.json' relative to the cwd; ops2/loop_ro.py reads part*.jsonl, win.jsonl, launches_now.json from the cwd (must be run inside money/).
- ops/lch/sim.py, ops2/sim.py, ops2/sim_copy.py, ops/lch/decode.py need eth_abi and eth_utils; ops2/copies_create.py needs rlp; ops2/sim.py also needs launcher_txs.json.
- ops2/verify3/vlib.py keeps its own fetched blocks in verify3/blk.pkl (missing); third/blk3.py in third/blk/ (missing); third/scan_cache.py writes cache_<lo>_<hi>.json (missing); all are rebuilt by RPC fetches.
- ops2/verify3/base.py and basefollow.py call the Base mainnet RPC https://mainnet.base.org (nonce binary search, hundreds of calls per recipient).
- The 21 ops2/verify3/relay/*.json files came from api.relay.link GET /requests/v2 (deprecated, rate-limited: 6 files are rate-limit error bodies, base_test.json is a Cloudflare challenge page, rs_test.json an explorer 'chain not supported' reply); no script in the repository produced them.
- third/ex_hist.py and third/fac_logs.py are whole-history log scans (20 x 1M-block and 232 x 250k-block eth_getLogs windows); third/classify.py, links.py, curvepath.py, sample.py, creators9.py, verify/v1.py all need the RPC (receipts, eth_getCode, logs) besides the cache.
- ops/scan.py writes ops/idx/*.json.gz (missing); ops/bprof.py writes ops/prof/ (missing); ops/deployers.py writes deployers_fund.json (missing); none of these outputs were copied.

**Open questions**

- Did loop B stop its Uniswap v4 launches after Oct 9 10:48 and move permanently to the bonding-curve platform (ops/lists.json last 4 curves, third/t6.json 3.2 ETH launch 11:51:30)? If so, the 'B' family in the engine's follow strategy may no longer appear on v4 and the fingerprint census should be re-checked.
- Why do A and B launch wallets call the bonding-curve platform 0xe33e9e47 (0xf85f8e41) immediately after each v4 launch, and what do the curve calls 0xd04c6983/0x3729bb9a and 0xd3afeb2a 0x4e71d92d do (claim/list)? Is the bonding-curve side profitable for the operator and is it tradable for us?
- Pot D (32 wallets swept to 0xdef59ef1, linked to A through 0x3ee51814 and 0xb63b68dc) is not covered in HANDOFF 6.4; is it part of operator A (origins/ may answer) and does its 3.99 ETH leg (0x0c8c381e -> 0x8ef3c8f1) feed a loop we have not seen?
- The handoff's 'at least 2, most likely 3 separate pots' rests on plain-ETH mixing tests; given that A, D and the Base collector 0x487cab40 (and Relay.link as the common bridge) connect several pots off-chain, is the separate-owner conclusion still defensible?
- What funded the B dispatcher of the Oct 9 09:24 launch (tr/0x758c3c5f.json found no incoming in 20,000 blocks) and the 9.72 ETH B sweep to 0x95295922 at 07:56 Oct 9 (bcrowd/gapscan.jsonl) - is there a second B reservoir?
- Who are the 12 'F candidate' Relay recipients (verify3/relay/f_*.json, 0.019-0.44 ETH at 12:25-12:49 Oct 8) and do they tie the fresh pot F to the same exchange account 0x487cab40? The basefollow run covered only the 8 A/D/B recipients.
- The copy-scheme collector's latest nonce is 791 vs 789 known (ops2/latest.json): the 2 later txs are the 6.1803 exit and one dust transfer; has it moved again since head 84,225,245?
- Is the launcher's permissionless launch() (ops2/sim.out) exploitable or useful for us (e.g. to test the engine against a launch we control), and does predictTokenAddress let the engine pre-compute the token/pool of an incoming launch before it lands?
- The third-system executor dumps on a schedule (delays 63-72,973 blocks, median ~800) into pools where crowd buys are 0.5-1.5 ETH and outsiders put in at most 0.11 ETH per token; is there a front-run or follow trade on the executor's dump that the parked research never priced?
- Relay.link deposits in cache total 842.98 ETH over Oct 8-9 (ops2/dep4cd.json); how much of that is other launch operators cashing out, and could the deposit stream be used as a signal of who is active?

## others-census

research/session_oct9_10/others/ is the census of every Uniswap v4 pool with ETH as currency0 launched on Robinhood Chain between Oct 8 00:00 UTC (block 82,878,144) and Oct 9 ~14:48 UTC (block 84,238,619): fetch.py found 5,164 Initialize logs from 4,108 launch transactions calling 235 distinct launch contracts, and pulled the Swap/ModifyLiquidity logs of each pool over [launch, launch+3000] (5,152 pools had a complete window; fetch.log). analyze.py (not runnable here: census.jsonl and every cache_*.pkl were not copied) derives per pool the engine's gates (hooks, fee >= 1%, liquidity < 1e21, no trigger within 150 blocks, 'late' trigger at <= +6), the first real buy (>= 0.0002 ETH), the pull, and a cheap paper follow trade (stake 0.0012 ETH, TP +1%, hold 2 or 30, land 1/2, fast/slow); families.py groups the pools into 625 launch families by launch contract (or code-size class) + selector + calldata length + fee tier + tick spacing + launch value (families.json): 391 singletons, 57 families with >= 8 pools, 8 with >= 100, and only 7 families contain any engine-tradable pool (840 pools: v2.433 529, PM2276 164, OURS-orig 86, OURS-copy 49, three tiny ones); gate counts over 5,152 pools are fee 3,561, no_trig 2,704, liq 2,161, hooks 670, late 274. The eight largest families are the volume bot's PM1444 v3.0 (718 pools, a fresh deployer each, 61% never pulled within 3,000 blocks), a 54,500-byte >1% family (700 pools, 5 deployers), PM1444 v2.433 (532), v5.0 (390), a 46,788-byte family (300), PMdirect 0x6276cbbe (204, all liquidity-gated), PM2276 (190) and a hooked family (127); operator A's original launcher is 9th with 94 pools (92 nonce-0 deployers, crowd trigger at +43/53/76 blocks p10/50/90) and its Oct 8 copy scheme 15th with 49 (trigger at +8/9, all pulled at +399..+1487). The paper follow (families.json land1_fast, hold 2) is +2.96% on OURS-orig (86 pools, 69 positive, 0 pulled), -0.32% on OURS-copy (1 of 49 positive), -1.77% on v2.433 (5 pulled), +0.44% on PM2276 (13 of 164 positive, outlier-driven), and -2.34% (pure fee loss) on v3.0/v5.0 which the fee gate excludes; variants.json extends the hold: OURS-orig +2.92% at hold 2 and +3.60% at hold 10 with 2 of 88 pulled, OURS-copy only positive from hold 10 (+2.24% at hold 20), v3.0 +4.73% at hold 30 (5 pulled of 711), v2.433 -1.11% at hold 30 with 44 pulled (median +8.6%), the ca11bde0 multicall3 family +1.9% at hold 10-30 on 19 pools. crowd.json samples the real buyers of 30 families: operator A's crowd (57 wallets in 40 pools, 31 recurring, 96.9% carrying the 1,124-byte/0.02 gwei Universal Router signature) shares zero wallets and zero signature hits with every other family; the PM1444 families are bought by one bot wallet (nonce 35,875-55,391) through 0x1cbaf24d with 324-byte calldata; crowdtrace.json finds 115 buyer wallets on the 94 original-launcher pools, 65 recurring (32 of them in 44-70 pools), and every token the PoolManager ever sent those 65 wallets (83 tokens, 7,364 buys) belongs to OURS-orig; sigsweep.json checks the first two real buys of 2,208 non-operator pools and finds 0 signature or crowd-wallet hits. That is the basis of the handoff's finding that no other v4 operator runs the follow pattern. others/ctl/ (not mentioned in handoff 6.5) re-prices the 9 Oct 9 morning live follow trades with the exact feed replayer (ctl_follow.json: paper within 0.13 pp of live on all 9, live net mean +3.75%, our buy at trigger+1 in 8 of 9, sell 2-3 blocks later) and dissects the 94-pool family (famanalyze.json: 70 scheme-A, 7 scheme-B, 13 other-crowd, 4 no-crowd pools; 81 pulls all by the deployer at nonce 1 through the PositionManager selector 0xdd46508f, 13 never pulled; 7,119 of 7,151 real buys before the pull carry the crowd signature; the 7 Oct 8 live pools were pulled 5-13 blocks after our buy); live_relay.json lists our 36 relay swaps on 18 OURS-orig pools up to Oct 9 ~15:40 UTC. others/verify/ is an independent verifier: 26 pools re-fetched fresh from the chain match the census row and cache on every field (v1_check.json, swap-replay error <= 6.3e-4), an independent sequential pricer (vprice.py) reproduces the census pricer within 0.13 pp (v2_price.json: OURS-orig arm6/hold2 +2.89%, 70 of 88 positive, 0 pulled, +2.44% without the top 5; v3.0 fixed entry at +16..+25 with hold 40 +5.4-5.6% with 1 pulled of 713; v2.433 fixed+25/hold40 +2.62% but 33 pulled of 520), a 181-pool v3.0 transaction sample shows every one of 17 outsider buys answered by a pull 4-13 blocks later (v3_analyze.json), reaction delays per PM1444 family (v5_delays.json: v3 median 9 blocks, 98 of 105 within 20; v5 median 16; v2.433 bimodal median 33), and a 1,180-pool out-of-sample window of ~6.9 h after the census (v6_post.json) in which the engine rule prices +3.17% on 17 new OURS-orig pools (12 positive), -0.85% on 101 v2.433 pools, and v3.0's hold-30 collapses to -10.45% with 63 of 166 pools pulled within 400 blocks. Only fetch.py runs as copied (against the RPC); everything else needs census.jsonl and the caches, and the ctl scripts hardcode the old scratchpad path.

**Numbers to remember**

- Census window: block 82,878,144 (Oct 8 00:00 UTC) to 84,238,619 (Oct 9 ~14:48 UTC); 5,164 v4 ETH-currency0 pools; 4,108 launch txs; 235 launch contracts; 5,152 pools with a complete 3,000-block log window (fetch.log)
- 625 launch families; 391 singletons; 57 with >= 8 pools; 8 with >= 100; 7 families hold all 840 engine-tradable pools (v2.433 529, PM2276 164, OURS-orig 86, OURS-copy 49, PM2020 ts60 4, 0xd0fd42bd 6, PM2020 ts1 2); 2,174 pools with a real trigger at +7..+150 (families.json)
- Gates over 5,152 pools: fee >= 1% 3,561; no trigger 2,704; liquidity < 1e21 2,161; hooks 670; late (trigger <= +6) 274; 3,067 distinct deployers; 197 nonce-0 launches (families.json)
- PM1444 v3.0: 718 pools, 718 deployers, 437 never pulled within 3,000 blocks, pull p10/50/90 +120/+468/+2,465, first real buy +8/+9/+13, median 12.36 ETH bought before the pull; v2.433: 532 pools, pull +27/+121/+1,237, 348 never pulled, 7.83 ETH; v5.0: 390, pull +131/+574/+2,260, 213 never pulled, 9.35 ETH; PM2276: 190, trigger +11/+32/+102, pull +154/+345/+2,038 (families.json)
- OURS-orig: 94 pools, 92 deployers, 92 nonce-0 launches, fees 0.021-0.49%, trigger p10/50/90 +43/+53/+76, pull +105/+816/+2,541, 13 never pulled in 3,000, median 2.58 ETH in before the pull, 86 tradable (families.json)
- OURS-copy: 49 pools, 49 deployers, launch nonce median 1, blocks 83,402,323-83,479,720 (Oct 8 only), trigger +8/+9/+9, pull +399/+1,461/+1,487 in all 49, 0.93 ETH in before the pull (families.json); 0 copy launches in the 6.9 h post-census window (v6_post.json)
- Paper follow hold 2, land 1 fast, 0.0012 ETH: OURS-orig +2.96% (86 pools, 69 positive, 0 pulled, worst -1.25%, best +12.1%); OURS-copy -0.32% (1 of 49 positive); v2.433 -1.77% (5 pulled); PM2276 +0.44% (13 of 164 positive); v3.0/v5.0 -2.34% (fee loss) (families.json)
- OURS-orig by hold: +2.92% (2), +3.57% (5), +3.60% (10), +3.61% (20/30/60); pulled 0/1/2/2/2/2 of 88 (variants.json); independent pricer +2.89% at hold 2 (70 of 88 positive, +2.44% without the top 5), +3.59% at hold 30 (v2_price.json)
- OURS-copy by hold: -0.32% (2, 5), +0.22% (10), +2.24% (20), +1.97% (30), +1.02% (60), 0 pulled (variants.json); with ARM 9 only 4 of 49 copy pools would trigger (v2_price.json)
- PM1444 v3.0 hold 30 +4.73% (668 of 711 positive, 5 pulled), hold 60 +5.04%; fixed entry +16..+25 with hold 40 +5.35..+5.57% with 1 pulled of 713 (variants.json, v2_price.json); out of sample Oct 9 evening hold 30 -10.45% on 165 pools, 63 of 166 pulled within 400 blocks (v6_post.json)
- PM1444 v2.433 follow: hold 2 -1.77% (5 pulled), hold 30 -1.11% (44 pulled, median +8.6%), hold 60 +0.51% (45 pulled); fixed+25/hold40 +2.62% mean, +9.92% median, 33 pulled of 520; 6-hour buckets of hold 30 range -17.3% to +7.0% (variants.json, v2_price.json); post-census hold 30 +6.35% (n 101), hold 2 -0.85% (0 positive), 27 of 102 pulled within 400 (v6_post.json)
- PM2276: hold 2 +0.44% (median -0.37%, -0.13% without the top 5, top pool +49.3%); 0.01 ETH trigger hold 2 +1.97% (n 119, +1.00% w/o top 5), hold 30 +4.06% (+2.63% w/o top 5) (v2_price.json); post-census 60 pools +1.44% (12 positive) (v6_post.json)
- Crowd sampling (crowd.json): 30 families; OURS-orig 40 pools / 352 buys / 57 wallets / 31 recurring / 96.9% with the 1,124-byte 0.02-gwei UR signature / buyer nonce median 1,523; OURS-copy 72 buys by 72 fresh wallets (nonce 0), 100% signature, 0 shared with OURS-orig; every other family 0 shared wallets, 0 signature; PM1444 buyer = one bot wallet (nonce 35,875-55,391) through 0x1cbaf24d, 324-byte calldata, 0.024 gwei
- Crowd trace (crowdtrace.json): 115 wallets on the 94 original-launcher pools, 65 in >= 2 pools, 32 in 44-70 pools; all 83 tokens the PoolManager sent those 65 wallets are OURS-orig, 7,364 buys; 0 from any other family

- Verifier (v1_check.json): 26 pools re-fetched fresh: 0 field mismatches vs census row and cache; swap replay error <= 6.3e-4; independent pricer within 0.13 pp of the census pricer
