**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 6.1 says 'reviews of releases 0.6, 0.8 and 0.9': the verify_engine/verify_live/verify_backtest/verify_adversarial scripts review the pre-0.6 engine (0.3-0.5) and the Oct 8 research (kind-4 blocks on the Oct 8 trades, the 8-trade reprice, the 0.3 exit backtest) — the review that produced 0.6; no directory reviews release 0.8 (5827baf) itself; release 0.9 was reviewed twice (48af392 -> 506a254 by verify_dstop/verify_slot/verify_wd/vfy_lookalike/vdstop3/vlate/vpnl/score_mix/rv09money/chk/repro; 7d1f645 -> 50bd0aa by rv/). The 'verify_*' glob in 6.1 therefore spans two unrelated reviews (files: verify_engine/single.py and timing_sim.py vs verify_dstop/repro.py, verify_wd/repro.py).
- HANDOFF 6.1 'the three 0.9 fix commits (section 8)': section 8 labels only two commits '0.9 fixes' (506a254) and '0.9 fixes 2' (50bd0aa); the third is unnamed (7d1f645 and 32416c1 sit unlabeled between/after them). The rv/ scripts were written against 7d1f645 (rv/flip_engine.py = 7d1f645), so the second 0.9 review's fixes are 50bd0aa only.
- HANDOFF 6.1 'Their confirmed findings became releases 0.6/0.8 (lost buy replies, refused sells, the keepalive holding the sell's connection, a launcher trusted by its call alone, the nonce after a failed settle)': the biggest 0.6 finding, blocks with one transaction arriving as bare kind-4 messages (verify_engine/single.py, k4.py; c2d9fcb's first bullet), is missing from this list; 'refused sells' names SELL_TRIES=4, which the review06_fixes/repro.py scenario B showed gives up within ~0.2 s — never changed; the 0.8 fixes also include the daily cap given back and the getCode re-read at the launch block (review06_regressions/tests/probe_cap.py, review06_refute_getcode/rg_stdout.txt), not listed.
- HANDOFF 6.1 'The directories hold the agents' scratch reproductions' and README line 4 'the review agents' directories keep their scripts and short text outputs only': almost no outputs exist — only review06_tests/*.txt, review06_refute_getcode/rg_stdout.txt, review06_lc/ops.txt and review06_refute_getcode/copies.txt; out_stdout.txt and sim_stdout.txt are empty; no verdict (confirmed/refuted) is written anywhere in the area, so the handoff's 'confirmed' attributions rest on the fix commits alone.
- HANDOFF 12.2/16.7 (and 8's ea66cc2 row): the safe-stop review 'was running; its verdict goes into section 16.7' — no directory, script, note or output for a review of on_stop/stop_tick/STOP_WAIT_S exists anywhere under research/session_oct9_10 (grep over the whole tree), and the HANDOFF at the latest commit 4dce46e still reads 'Running at the time of writing'. The review never completed and nothing of it was kept.
- research/session_oct9_10/README.md's review row lists a `tests/` directory among the review scratch dirs: no such directory exists. The README also lists rt/, repro/ and chk/ as review scratch (they are: results.py probes, the phantom-TP repro, the start-checks repro), but HANDOFF 6.1's heading names only review06_*, verify_*, rv*, vfy_*, vdstop3, vlate, vpnl, score_mix.
- HANDOFF 8 row 506a254 lists 'sqrtP bounds' among the fixes and row 7d1f645 says the fingerprint is 'logged, not required': the look-alike finding (vfy_lookalike/repro.py, rv09money/scen3.py: any sender's front-shaped launch with a fake price and fee traded at the fake price) was fixed in 506a254 by requiring the whole fingerprint, then re-opened by design in 7d1f645 with FRONT_PIN=0 (7.3 shows FRONT_PIN 0 on the box); the handoff never says the review's look-alike finding is thereby accepted as a residual risk.
- HANDOFF 8 row 506a254 omits two fixes the review scripts motivated and the commit made: 'a pool is armed once' (vfy_lookalike's re-armed pid) and the liq-0 look-alike that raised out of ingest and lost the block's follow trigger (rv09money/scen2.py) — covered in the commit message ('nothing in the front path can raise out of the feed') and tests but not in the table.
- HANDOFF 7.4 'Run 22 times in a row on Oct 10 without a failure after test 15j was made nonce-exact': the only stress transcripts kept are review06_tests' 13 runs of the 20-test 0.6 suite (Oct 9); the 22 Oct 10 runs left no file in the area.

**Open questions**

- reply_kind() still raises on a reply whose 'error' is a string, None, a list or a non-dict (review06_regr/rk.py; review06_concurrency/c2d9fcb/tests/probe_strerr.py showed the sell's deliver_sell thread dying before settle and the position staying open for good); src/flip_engine.py's reply_kind and deliver_sell are unchanged since c2d9fcb and deliver_sell has no try/except. Was this refuted as unreachable (the Sender turns non-JSON bodies into message-only dicts, and JSON-RPC servers return error objects), or simply dropped? No verdict is kept.
- _oneoff()'s error path does not close its connection (review06_regressions/leak.py); unchanged at HEAD. Presumably refuted (CPython closes the socket on garbage collection) but no output or note says so.
- The look-alike launch finding (vfy_lookalike, rv09money/scen3): 506a254 required the full fingerprint; 7d1f645 made fee/tick-spacing/price/liquidity logged-only and FRONT_PIN is 0 on the box. A copycat launch with the bot's 2.4333285 ETH value and 1444-byte multicall shape, a sub-1% fee, in-range liquidity and any in-bounds price is traded live at the front stake. Is that an accepted residual risk (bounded by the stake and the halts), and should FRONT_PIN=1 be the live default?
- SELL_TRIES=4 with 0.05 s pauses (review06_fixes/repro.py scenario B: the sell given up within ~0.2 s on fast transient refusals, never re-sent, the 'sold none' halt afterwards): was keeping it an explicit decision? The handoff's 'refused sells' fix (6.1) refers to retrying at all, not to this.
- The safe-stop review (ea66cc2, handoff 12.2/16.7): did it ever produce findings? Nothing exists in the repository; if its scratch survives in the second session's scratchpad it should be copied in, otherwise ea66cc2 remains tested but unreviewed.
- Which commit is the 'third 0.9 fix commit' of HANDOFF 6.1 — 7d1f645 (fingerprint relaxed) or 32416c1 (sell on the bot's buy)? Neither is labeled in section 8.
- rv/s1.py (a live front at L, a seat launch at L+1: the seat never gets the slot) and rv/s5_verify.py cases E/F (seat launches during a front trade) have no recorded outcome; the yield gate at HEAD covers seat pools only while they wait (status armed, first_buy None) — was the seat-vs-front ordering judged acceptable?
- The verify_live reprice and verify_backtest variants A-J have no surviving outputs: the engine docstring's eight repriced returns and the handoff's Oct 8/9 paper numbers cannot be re-checked from the area without re-running against the RPC.
- review06_correctness (type-4 / EIP-7702 transactions in recent blocks and among early swaps): did any appear, and does parse_tx handle them? No output kept.
- The review06_fixes/repro.py scenario C and the live measurement (4/30 empty reads at a 7-block gap) imply the 0.8 re-read at the launch block covers the node-behind case; was a node that is still behind at the launch block's own tag (fetch(rec['blockNumber'])) ever observed? live_probe.py's out/ would have said; it is gone.
- others/census.jsonl was not copied (size), so vlate/early.py (how often a >= 0.0002 ETH buy lands in the launch block or the next two on the v2.433 family — the basis for FRONT_AHEAD_MAX 0.008) cannot be re-run from the repository.

## deploy-contracts-src

The area holds the box's operating scripts (/home/user/v2pons/deploy, 9 files), the FlipRelay contract and its compiled artifact (/home/user/v2pons/contracts, 4 files), the exact Uniswap v4 single-range pricer (src/v4math.py, 59 lines, 4 tests all passing) and the follow-only after-the-fact scorer (src/score.py). FlipRelay.sol (solc 0.8.26, optimizer 1000 runs, cancun; runtime 5,219 bytes, creation 5,470) has 7 external functions: buy(key,amountIn,minOut,deadline) 5ed06f60, sell(key,minOut,deadline) 137f1fdd, unlockCallback(bytes) 91dd7346, withdraw(uint256) 2e1a7d4d (0 = everything), sweep(address) 01681a62, owner() 8da5cb5b, pm() 8970cdff, plus receive(); 8 custom errors (NotOwner 30cd7471, NotPoolManager, NotEthPair, TooLate, Insufficient, TooLittleOut(uint256,uint256) e20a4c5d, NothingToSell cb72d3db, TransferFailed) and two require strings ("withdraw", "sweep"). The sell sends the relay's ENTIRE token balance (balanceOf at execution) into the pool with sqrtPriceLimit MAX_SQRT_PRICE-1 and the engine always passes minOut=0 and deadline=0 (flip_engine.py tx_sell line 600), so by the contract's logic a sell into a fully pulled pool does NOT revert: v4's swap returns delta (0,0), the callback's sign checks pass, out=0 >= minOut 0, the token transfer and take are skipped, and the call returns 0 with the tokens still in the relay; it reverts only if the relay holds zero tokens (NothingToSell, e.g. after a reverted buy) or if minOut>0. In practice (HANDOFF 16.1) the two Oct 10 post-pull sells reverted after 335,520 of 350,000 gas: the swap loop walks the tick bitmap word by word to the price limit (about 54 words at tick spacing 50) and runs out of SELL_GAS; the gas figure (about 350,000 minus the nested 1/64 reserves) is the out-of-gas signature, not a contract revert path. Either way the tokens stay in the relay (recoverable with relay_ops.py sweep <token>) and the engine's settle() halts on "the buy filled and the sell sold none of it" or on pulled_before_sell. compile.py writes FlipRelay.json with a source sha256 (14616699fe7c…) that matches the current .sol (verified), and relay_deploy.py refuses a stale artifact. flip_setup.sh's systemd unit sets only EnvironmentFile, WorkingDirectory=$REPO, ExecStart=/opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 $REPO/src/flip_engine.py, Restart=always, RestartSec=3, Nice=-5: NO TimeoutStopSec and NO KillMode, so the safe stop (STOP_WAIT_S 40 s) relies on systemd's defaults (DefaultTimeoutStopSec 90 s, KillMode=control-group, SIGTERM then SIGKILL); the script also pip-installs on every run and ends with an unconditional systemctl restart (on the box's cd3d1c9 engine, which has no on_stop, that abandons an open trade). relay_ops.py (status/deposit/withdraw/sweep/payout) refuses everything but status while the service is active, signs legacy txs at gasPrice 2x and gas 1.3x estimate on chain 4663, keeps a 0.003 ETH gas reserve on payout and validates the destination checksum; its status counts "today" from the unrotated log only. relay_deploy.py deploys with the PoolManager as constructor arg, checks the runtime byte for byte with the immutables (AST ids 71=pm 11 refs, 73=owner 7 refs) filled in, reads owner()/pm(), then simulates a 1e14-wei buy on the operator's latest unpulled pool and only afterwards writes RELAY= with --write-env; because latest_operator_pool()/pool_state() raise SystemExit (not Exception), a missing operator pool aborts the script before the env write. relay_sim.py does a full buy+sell round trip through eth_call state overrides with RoundTripHarness as owner and tests minOut enforcement, but its TooLittleOut check looks for selector "d06c7f4b" (the real one is e20a4c5d) and falls back to any "revert" string, so the "TooLittleOut ok" line is weak. results.py (stdlib only, run with sudo) reads every rotation, dedupes trade_done by (t, nonce), prints the header from the last start line (follow cap from max_trades_per_day, front cap from front_max_per_day), the PROFIT BY STRATEGY table, pulls after our sell per strategy, $ a trade by stake, launches, skip reasons over the whole window (the "daily cap" reason does not say which strategy), front's last 5 rows, FRONT LIVE/DRY RUN summaries, alarms and balances; HEAD adds 4 lines for the follow halt that the owner's ~/results.py (4e8d350) lacks. score.py prices follow decisions from the chain with the release-0.3 exit and reads two fields (tokens_left, sell_amount_src) the 0.9 engine never logs. v4math.py is validated to 6e-15 median relative error on 33,769 buys and 2e-5 on 26,720 sells; the fixture tests/data/front_launch.json is the real v2.433 launch tx 0xe1d91366… in block 84234302 (1,444-byte multicall, 2.4333285 ETH, fee 2500, ts 50, sqrtP 1604517591721945638372359205383840, tick 198329, liq 49279426163460954002188, full range -887250..887250). The engine's test suite printed 46 ok at HEAD (consistent with HANDOFF 7.4; the task text's 44 is the count at 3c3f696). On the owner's request (remove front's daily cap): nothing in this area needs to change; the cap is FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY (code default 400, no 0=unlimited value, gate at flip_engine.py line 645) set in /etc/flip/engine.env with set_kv and a restart, and results.py will show the new value in its front line and stop printing "daily cap" skips.

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 9.1 (and 11.2) state the unit's ExecStart as '/opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 ~/v2pons/src/flip_engine.py'; deploy/flip_setup.sh writes the absolute path $REPO/src/flip_engine.py (systemd does not expand ~). Cosmetic.
- HANDOFF 11.2 says "systemd's TimeoutStopSec is 90 s" as if configured; deploy/flip_setup.sh's unit sets no TimeoutStopSec and no KillMode, so 90 s is systemd's DefaultTimeoutStopSec (Ubuntu default), which /etc/systemd/system.conf on the box could override. The safe stop's 40 s margin depends on that default.
- HANDOFF 2 (repository table) lists relay_ops.py as 'status, deposit, withdraw, sweep' and omits payout, which the file has (c9d0201) and which sections 9.2, 11.3 and 16.6 describe. The table was not updated.
- HANDOFF 7.1 says a sell after a pull is handled as 'a filled buy whose sell sold nothing -> halt', and 16.1 says the two post-pull sells 'reverted (335,520 of 350,000 gas ...)'. contracts/FlipRelay.sol has no revert path for an empty pool when minOut is 0 (the engine's value): the observed revert is the SELL_GAS limit running out in the bitmap walk, not the contract. Both documents are consistent with the chain, but neither says that a larger SELL_GAS would make the sell succeed with 0 ETH out (tokens left in the relay, sweep needed) rather than revert.
- docs/RUNBOOK.md section 0 claims the relay simulation proved 'minOut enforced' and 4c expects 'TooLittleOut ok'; deploy/relay_sim.py prints 'TooLittleOut ok' on ANY revert string because its selector constant d06c7f4b is not TooLittleOut's (e20a4c5d). The relay does enforce minOut (source), but the simulation's check does not prove which error fired.
- HANDOFF 2 says v4math is 'validated to 6e-15 median relative error on 33,769 real buys' (matches src/v4math.py); README.md line 125 says 'validated to 1e-13 on 60,000 real swaps' (the buy and sell counts summed, a different error figure). README vs file, not handoff vs file.
- HANDOFF 16.2 (chain read): the pull came 1-3 blocks after our sell in 398 of 400 front trades, 2 blocks in 363; the owner's results.py screen (from the same trade_done fields) shows 1 block x11, 2 x360, 3 x24 = 395 within 1-3 blocks, plus 11 blocks x1, none within 30 x2, before x2. Small count difference (3 trades) between the two sources, unexplained.
- HANDOFF 15 and the computed task say 'tests/test_engine.py (44 tests must pass)'; HANDOFF 2 and 7.4 say 46 at head; running it here gave 46 ok. 44 is the count at 3c3f696 (7.4 explains this), so the prompt in section 15 is stale rather than wrong.
- HANDOFF 9.3 says results.py's 'not traded:' line shows skip reasons; the 'daily cap' reason is the same text for front and follow (flip_engine.py line 646 logs the strategy only in the count key), so the owner's 'daily cap x60' cannot be attributed to front from the screen alone (it is front's, since follow had 5 of 100). The handoff does not mention the ambiguity.
- No file named in HANDOFF sections 2, 7, 9 is missing: all of deploy/{flip_setup.sh,results.py,relay_ops.py,relay_deploy.py,relay_sim.py,new_wallet.py,englog.py,health.sh,watch.sh}, contracts/{FlipRelay.sol,FlipRelay.json,compile.py,RoundTripHarness.sol}, src/{v4math.py,score.py}, tests/test_v4math.py and tests/data/front_launch.json exist.

**Scripts that do not run as copied**

- deploy/results.py, relay_ops.py, relay_deploy.py, englog.py, health.sh, watch.sh hardcode /etc/flip/engine.env, /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl and /var/log/flip/HALTED(.front/.follow); they run only on a box set up by flip_setup.sh and (for the env file, mode 600 root) only under sudo. results.py ignores the env's LOG_PATH/HALT_FILE overrides.
- deploy/relay_ops.py, relay_deploy.py, new_wallet.py, src/score.py need eth_account/eth_utils: they must run with /opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 (system python3 fails on import). results.py, englog.py, watch.sh and health.sh's log part are stdlib only and work with 'sudo python3'.
- deploy/relay_sim.py and contracts/compile.py need py-solc-x and download solc 0.8.26 (about 10 MB) from the internet on first use; relay_sim also needs an RPC that accepts eth_call state overrides (the public endpoint does, per the runbook).
- deploy/relay_deploy.py: with --write-env, if the operator has no unpulled pool (SystemExit from relay_sim.latest_operator_pool/pool_state, not caught by 'except Exception'), the script exits after the on-chain deploy and before writing RELAY= to the env file; the address is only in the printed output.
- deploy/relay_ops.py status counts 'today' from /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl only: after the daily logrotate (copytruncate) trades logged earlier the same UTC day are missing from that count (results.py reads the rotations and is correct).
- deploy/flip_setup.sh requires root, network (pip install every run), and ends with an unconditional 'systemctl restart flip-engine': on the box's cd3d1c9 engine (no on_stop) that kills an open trade; on ea66cc2+ it waits up to STOP_WAIT_S.
- deploy/health.sh reports only /var/log/flip/HALTED; a front-only (HALTED.front) or follow-only (HALTED.follow) halt is invisible in it (results.py shows them).
- src/score.py reads only the one log file passed (no rotations), re-fetches eth_getLogs per decision from the public RPC (429-prone), and ignores front; it is not broken but is a follow-only, pre-0.9 tool.
- deploy/relay_sim.py's TooLittleOut check uses a wrong selector constant (d06c7f4b) and therefore passes on any revert message; a relay that reverted for another reason would still print 'TooLittleOut ok'.
- The research scripts (research/session_oct9_10/**) are outside this area but the README there states they embed /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad and /home/user/v2pons and will not run as copied.

**Open questions**

- Is the observed post-pull sell revert really out-of-gas in the tick-bitmap walk (335,520 of 350,000 gas at SELL_GAS 350000), and would raising SELL_GAS make it succeed with 0 ETH out (tokens stranded in the relay, requiring relay_ops.py sweep) or is there an Arbitrum/L1-posting gas component that changes the picture? Nobody has run a post-pull sell through relay_sim.py against a pulled pool.
- Are there any tokens stranded in the relay from the two Oct 10 -100% trades (and the three Oct 8 pulled-first trades)? relay_ops.py sweep <token> would recover them if the pool ever regains liquidity; nothing in the area checks the relay's token balances.
- Does the box's /etc/systemd/system.conf keep DefaultTimeoutStopSec at 90 s? The safe stop (STOP_WAIT_S 40) assumes it; the unit does not pin TimeoutStopSec. Adding TimeoutStopSec=90 and KillMode=mixed/control-group explicitly in flip_setup.sh would remove the assumption (a code change, not done here).
- The owner wants front's daily cap removed: the engine has no 'unlimited' value (gate >= cap at flip_engine.py line 645), so the only lever is set_kv FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY <large number> plus a restart (on cd3d1c9 the restart must wait for an open trade, HANDOFF 9.2). Should a 0 = no cap value be added to the engine and results.py's header? Also results.py's 'daily cap' skip reason does not say which strategy hit it.
- Why do the handoff's chain-read pull offsets (398 of 400 at 1-3 blocks after our sell, 363 at 2) differ by 3 trades from results.py's trade_done-based counts (395; 360 at 2) on the owner's screen?
- What does 'd06c7f4b' in relay_sim.py refer to? No FlipRelay version in git history had a different TooLittleOut signature; the constant appears from the first commit and is probably a typo.
- relay_ops.py send() gives up after 60 s and prints 'status None' while the transaction may still land later; no nonce/receipt follow-up is offered. Is that acceptable for payout?
- score.py still references release 0.2 fields (tokens_left, sell_amount_src) and excludes front; should it be retired or extended now that results.py is the primary tool?

## tests-vs-engine

The checkout at /home/user/v2pons is branch claude/laughing-fermat-48ylv7 at commit 4dce46e, four commits past the 3c3f696 the task text names (3a47c34 router rule, c9d0201 payout, 792a8c5 near-miss halt, 4dce46e handoff 16); remote claude/pensive-rubin-rkj996 still ends at 3c3f696, so the two branches are not identical. At this head tests/test_engine.py is 835 lines with 46 `print("ok ...")` lines (not 787/44 as the task text says; those are 3c3f696's numbers, and the box runs cd3d1c9 with 766 lines/43 tests) and src/flip_engine.py is 1208 lines (not 1182). I ran the suite three times (RPC, sender and signer are stubbed inside the test file; nothing touches the network): 46 ok each time, exit 0, about 8.9 s wall; tests/test_v4math.py prints 4 ok. The test file is one linear script of 46 scenario blocks (numbered 1-5, 9-18o; there are no blocks 6-8) that feeds real RLP-signed legacy transactions in real L2 batch frames through E.ingest and asserts on E.state, on the SENT raw transactions and on the JSONL log; a wrapper run that counted the log shows the 46 tests emit 44 fire, 44 buy_reply, 29 trade_done (27 live + 2 dry), 27 sell_sent, 27 skip, 13 alarm, 72 armed, 76 launch, 52 first_buy, 28 buy_seen, 28 nonce, 4 would_fire, 2 would_sell, 2 stopping, 2 stopped, 1 buy_rejected, 1 launch_undecoded, 1 front_bot_reverts. The engine emits exactly 26 distinct `ev` names (matching handoff 7.2 one for one); 18 of them appear during the tests and 8 never do: start, flow, feed_connected, feed_error, feed_stall, launch_no_receipt, launch_no_pool, no_first_buy. deploy/results.py reads 10 of the 26 (start, flow, trade_done, armed, skip, fire, buy_rejected, would_fire, would_sell, alarm) and ignores 16 (feed_connected, feed_error, feed_stall, launch, launch_undecoded, launch_no_receipt, launch_no_pool, first_buy, buy_reply, buy_seen, sell_sent, nonce, no_first_buy, front_bot_reverts, stopping, stopped); health.sh and src/score.py read some of the ignored ones (first_buy, sell_sent, feed_error, feed_stall). Of the engine's 18 distinct skip reasons the tests exercise 16 (missing: "front: the bot's buy came (and reverts) before our fire" line 995 and "seat: armed too late to land ahead of the burst" line 1084); of its 13 alarm sites the tests exercise 6 (missing: front launch not armed (error), arming failed, fire failed, settle failed, nonce refresh failed, watchdog tick failed, engine crashed). The biggest structural gaps are: every test transaction is legacy (gasPrice), so parse_tx's type-1 and type-2 branches (lines 285-288) that decode almost all real feed traffic are never run; nothing exercises the real rpc(), the real Sender network methods, feed_loop, fee_loop, relay_balance, watchdog(), main(), the `flow`/prune path (needs 600 ingested frames; the tests ingest fewer), checks()'s seven SystemExit branches, settle()'s exception path and refresh_nonce, or chain_nonce exhausting its 60 tries. Directly relevant to the user's request: the front daily cap gate at lines 643-646 (`state["front_today"] >= FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY` -> skip "daily cap") is never reached by any test (test 17 sets FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY=50 but never accumulates 50; "daily cap" is tested only for follow in tests 2 and 18e), there is no "0 = unlimited" semantics (FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY=0 makes `0 >= 0` true and blocks every front trade), FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY is a code default (400, line 107) not present in /etc/flip/engine.env per handoff 7.3, and restore_today (lines 363-389, test 18n) re-reads today's 400 trade_done lines at a restart so a new cap must exceed 400 to trade again before 00:00 UTC. results.py shows follow's cap in its header ("max 100 trades a day" = MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY) and front's on the "front strategy:" line ("max 400 a day" from the start event's front_max_per_day); its "not traded: daily cap x60" cannot say which strategy hit the cap (it truncates `why` to 60 chars; the skip event itself carries "front_today": 400). The handoff's claims about the two files hold except two small ones: 7.4 says the 46 ok lines are "listed in the test file's docstring" but the docstring stops at the release-0.6 tests, and section 15 (line 737) still says "44 tests must pass".

**Numbers to remember**

- 46 ok lines from python3 tests/test_engine.py at 4dce46e, 3 consecutive passes, ~8.9 s wall each (tests/test_engine.py; my runs)
- 44 ok at 3c3f696 (787-line tests, 1182-line engine); 43 ok at cd3d1c9, the commit the box runs (766/1144 lines) (git show; docs/HANDOFF.md 7.4 and 11.1)
- 835 lines tests/test_engine.py and 1208 lines src/flip_engine.py at HEAD 4dce46e (wc -l)
- 4 ok from tests/test_v4math.py (tests/test_v4math.py)
- 26 distinct log event names in the engine; 18 emitted during the tests; 8 never (start, flow, feed_connected, feed_error, feed_stall, launch_no_receipt, launch_no_pool, no_first_buy) (src/flip_engine.py; wrapper run)
- 10 of 26 events read by deploy/results.py; 16 ignored (deploy/results.py)
- 18 distinct skip reasons in the engine, 16 exercised by tests; 13 alarm sites, 6 exercised (src/flip_engine.py; wrapper run)
- Event counts emitted by the 46 tests: fire 44, buy_reply 44, trade_done 29 (27 live, 2 dry), sell_sent 27, skip 27, alarm 13, armed 72, launch 76, first_buy 52, buy_seen 28, nonce 28, would_fire 4, would_sell 2, stopping 2, stopped 2, buy_rejected 1, launch_undecoded 1, front_bot_reverts 1 (wrapper run of tests/test_engine.py)
- FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY default 400, src/flip_engine.py line 107; gate lines 643-646; MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY default 10 (100 on the box) line 95
- FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY=0 would skip every front trade (0 >= 0), there is no unlimited value (src/flip_engine.py 643-646)
- restore_today re-reads today's live trade_done lines at start, so after the 400th trade a restart keeps front_today=400 (src/flip_engine.py 363-389; test 18n)

- SELL_TRIES 4, NONCE_TRIES 60, FRONT_FIRE_MAX 2, FRONT_HOLD_BLOCKS 18, FRONT_SELL_ON_ETH 0.001 (was 0.005 before 3a47c34), FRONT_BOT_MIN_ETH 0.04, FRONT_BOT_MINOUT 0.99, FRONT_AHEAD_MAX_ETH 0.008, FOLLOW_NEAR_MISS_BLOCKS 4, FRONT_DAILY_STOP_USD 5, STOP_WAIT_S 40 (src/flip_engine.py)

- User's results.py (10-10 13:05 UTC): front 400 trades today +$700.34, 98% up, +$1.751/trade, +13.5% avg, worst -100%; follow 5 today +$0.63, 42 since 10-08 -$4.76; launches seen 487 (follow 24, front 463); not traded 'daily cap x60'; 'BEFORE our sell x2 <== halts'; by stake $5: 60 trades +$41.50, $10: 23 +$31.07, $15: 317 +$627.78 (user message)
- Handoff 16.5: cap hit with the 11:39:54 UTC trade, nonce 893/894, wallet nonce 895; ~296 bot launches 10:00-13:07 (docs/HANDOFF.md 16.5)
- Handoff 16.6: wallet 0.002464 ETH (~200 trades of gas), relay 0.276414 ETH (~$710 at $2570); proposed set_kv FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY 1000 (docs/HANDOFF.md 16.6)
- Branch state: laughing-fermat at 4dce46e is 4 commits past 3c3f696 (3a47c34, c9d0201, 792a8c5, 4dce46e); remote pensive-rubin tip is 3c3f696 (git log, git ls-remote)
- Dependencies present here: Python 3.13.16, rlp 5.0.0, eth_account 0.14.0, eth_utils 6.0.0, websockets 17.2 (python3 -c import)

**Discrepancies with the handoff (the reader's claims; the critic verified the first four areas' ones)**

- HANDOFF 7.4 says the 46 ok lines are 'listed in the test file's docstring and by grep' — tests/test_engine.py lines 1-11 (the docstring) list only the scenarios of blocks 1-16 (through release 0.6: live path, gates, dry run, stale, reverted buy, exit, feed price, replay, halt, BUY_DELAY, seat, kind-4/nested, lost reply, refused buy, sell retry, time fallback, sold none, sender, look-alike launcher); the 23 release-0.9/front, restore_today and safe-stop tests (blocks 17-18o) are not listed there; only grep '^ok' gives 46.
- HANDOFF section 15 (line 737, 'The prompt for the next agent') says 'tests/test_engine.py (44 tests must pass)' while 7.4 and line 37 say 46 ok; 46 is correct at HEAD 4dce46e (44 was true at 3c3f696).
- HANDOFF 11.2 says 'git pull ... expect 44' for the box's pull of claude/pensive-rubin-rkj996 — consistent with that branch's remote tip 3c3f696 (44), but the install path that 16.6 actually gives (fast-forward onto laughing-fermat) yields 46; both numbers appear without the 11.2 one being marked stale.
- TASK TEXT (not the handoff) says the checkout is 'commit 3c3f696, identical content to claude/pensive-rubin-rkj996', with 44 tests, 787-line tests and 1182-line engine — the checkout is 4dce46e (835/1208 lines, 46 tests) and HANDOFF 16.6 itself states pensive-rubin 'still ends at 3c3f696', i.e. the branches differ by 4 commits; a future agent given the task text's numbers would look for a test count and line counts that do not exist on this branch.
- HANDOFF 9.3 says the results.py header shows 'caps' — the header line shows only follow's cap ('max 100 trades a day' = MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY); front's cap appears on the separate 'front strategy:' line; the user's reading of the header as the limit that stopped front is explained by this, not contradicted (deploy/results.py lines 150-153 and 173-176).
- No contradiction found between HANDOFF 7.2 (26 event names) and the engine, nor between 7.3's code defaults and src/flip_engine.py lines 66-137; every file named in sections 7, 16.1-16.6 and the engine's docstring exists (exits_bt.out, front_audit/full_audit.json, live_trades.py, live_trades_oct8.txt, FlipRelay.sol, score.py, englog.py, RUNBOOK.md, chain.py, money/loop.json, tests/data/front_launch.json).

**Scripts that do not run as copied**

- deploy/results.py hardcodes /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl (+ rotations), /etc/flip/engine.env, /var/log/flip/HALTED, HALTED.front, HALTED.follow, runs `systemctl is-active flip-engine` and calls the live RPC for balances: off the box it prints 'service STOPPED', 'no start line in the logs', 'money: WALLET or RELAY is not in /etc/flip/engine.env' and no tables; it needs the box's log and env (or those paths edited) and sudo to read the env file.
- deploy/englog.py and deploy/health.sh hardcode /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl and /opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 (health.sh also calls relay_ops.py status): box only.
- tests/test_engine.py needs third-party rlp, eth_account and eth_utils importable (present here: 5.0.0 / 0.14.0 / 6.0.0 under Python 3.13.16; the box uses /opt/flip-venv/bin/python3), needs tests/data/front_launch.json beside it, and writes tempfile.mktemp() paths (<tmp>.jsonl, .jsonl.halt, .halt.front, .halt.follow, .jsonl.day) that it removes itself; the test-run CWD does not matter (ROOT from __file__). It does not need websockets (imported only inside feed_loop), though the engine itself does at runtime.
- tests/test_engine.py test 18n (restore_today) wraps its two assertions in `if E.today() == time.strftime(..., time.gmtime(now_t))`: run across UTC midnight it prints ok without checking anything.
- tests/test_engine.py uses wall-clock waits (wait_for default 3 s, 4-6 s in 15d/15k; SYNC_BUY waits for the buy reply; settle_dry sleeps 1 s): timing-sensitive on a slow machine, though the handoff reports 22 consecutive passes and I saw 3/3.
- src/flip_engine.py at import time builds RELAY_TO = to_checksum_address(RELAY) only if RELAY is set and raises SystemExit if ENTRY is not follow/seat/auto; main() requires FEED_URL/SEQ_URL/RPC_URL reachability, PRIVATE_KEY when DRY_RUN=0 and a 4663 chain id — none of which the tests touch (they stub rpc/SENDER/SIGNER before any call).
- research/session_oct9_10/*: the README says every script embeds /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad and /home/user/v2pons and must be edited before running (outside this area; not run).

**Open questions**

- For the user's request ('remove the front daily limit'): the engine has no unlimited value — FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY=0 would block every front trade (lines 643-646), so 'remove' means either a very large env value (the handoff's 16.6 step 4 proposes 1000; nothing in the engine objects to e.g. 1000000) or a code change making 0 mean off plus a test, since no test currently exercises the front cap at all. Which does the orchestrator want?
- Because restore_today re-reads today's 400 live front trade_done lines at start, a restart with a cap of 400 or less changes nothing until 00:00 UTC; the new cap must exceed today's count (400 at 13:05 UTC). Is the box's log intact (no rotation gap) so that the restored count is the real one?
- FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY is not in /etc/flip/engine.env on the box (handoff 7.3, not bold), so the change is an added line, not an edit; the handoff's set_kv helper handles both. Has the box already pulled past cd3d1c9 (the user's output shows release 0.9 but nothing that distinguishes cd3d1c9 from 4dce46e)? At cd3d1c9 the expected test count is 43, at 4dce46e 46.
- With the cap lifted, gas is the next binding limit: the wallet held 0.002464 ETH (~200 trades) at 13:26 UTC per handoff 16.6 and front runs ~300-400 trades a day; repeated 'insufficient funds' refusals exercise deliver_buy's rejected path (test 15c, one refusal) and a chain_nonce RPC per refusal — untested at volume. Should the gas top-up (16.6 step 7) go with the cap change?
- FRONT_DAILY_STOP_USD ($5) and the pull-before-sell halt stay in force; with 400+ trades at a $15 stake, one -100% trade (two happened Oct 10) halts front regardless of the cap. Is that the intended remaining brake?
- The results.py 'not traded: daily cap x60' line merges follow's and front's caps (why truncated to 60 chars); the skip event itself carries 'front_today': N. Should results.py show the strategy, or is the handoff's 'front stopped at its cap' reading enough?
- Coverage: every test transaction is legacy (gasPrice), so parse_tx's type-1/type-2 branches that decode nearly all real feed traffic are untested; feed_loop, fee_loop, watchdog(), checks()'s SystemExits, settle()'s exception path, chain_nonce exhaustion and the `flow`/prune path are untested. Does the orchestrator want these listed as proposed tests, given 'do not modify any file'?
- Test 18n's assertions are skipped across UTC midnight and the suite depends on 3-6 s wall-clock waits; is a flakiness check (the handoff's 22 runs) wanted on the box's venv as well?

**Open questions for section 12**

- (The owner's actual request, 16.6 step 4 / 12.5) How to 'remove' FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY: the engine reads it as int(env) with no in-code bound and gates with `state['front_today'] >= cap` (flip_engine.py 643-646), so FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY=0 would skip every front trade ('daily cap'), not lift the cap; a very large value (e.g. 100000) is the only way to remove it. The counter is rebuilt from the day's live trade_done lines at start (restore_today, lines 363-389, reads engine.jsonl and .1), so a plain restart will not resume front today: the owner must `set_kv FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY <n>` and restart. The variable is not in the box's env file (HANDOFF 7.3 shows the default 400 in force; results.py's header line reads it from the start line). Open: what value, and whether the cap was the only bound on a bad day worth keeping (see the next item).
- FRONT_DAILY_STOP_USD defaults to $5 (flip_engine.py 113; the owner's screen shows 'its own daily stop $5.0') while the front stake is $15 (0.0058 ETH): the stop is cumulative P&L today <= -$5, so one -100% trade before about +$5 has accrued in a UTC day (the first 3-8 trades after 00:00 UTC, or after a restore) stops front for the rest of the day with 'daily stop' skips, whereas after a profitable morning (+$700 on Oct 10) the same loss trips nothing. Removing the trade cap makes this the only daily money bound besides the pull-before-sell halt; it should be set deliberately (it was sized for a $3-5 stake).

Also confirm the box can fetch the private repository (deploy key, 11.4) before raising the cap, since both changes need the same restart.

**Must know, not in the handoff**

- Where the code actually stands versus the box: this checkout is HEAD 4dce46e on claude/laughing-fermat-48ylv7 (46 tests), four commits past the 3c3f696 the task names; origin/claude/pensive-rubin-rkj996 still ends at 3c3f696; the box runs cd3d1c9 (43 tests) and its last start was 11:17:19 UTC Oct 10 (per the owner's screen), so none of the afternoon's fixes (FRONT_BOT_ROUTERS, FRONT_SELL_ON_ETH 0.001, the follow near-miss halt, payout) is live there: a 0.00495 ETH bot buy still costs the whole $15 stake until 16.6 steps 2-3 run. The owner's ~/results.py is a fetched copy at 4e8d350, not the repo's.
- The cap and stop mechanics behind 'front stopped at 400': FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY is int(env) with no in-code bound, compared with `>=` at fire time (so 0 means no front trades, not unlimited); front_today is incremented at every fire (dry runs included) and rebuilt at start from the day's live trade_done lines in engine.jsonl and engine.jsonl.1 (restore_today), so a restart never resets it and the day rolls only at 00:00 UTC; the variable is not in the box's env file (default 400), so lifting it is `set_kv FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY <large>` plus a restart. FRONT_DAILY_STOP_USD is $5 (cumulative today) against a $15 stake: one -100% in a day's first few trades stops front for the day, while after a profitable morning it never trips. FRONT_STAKE_ETH is hard-capped at 0.006 in code (min(..., 0.006)).
- Operator A's launcher is permissionless: ops/lch/sim_out.txt shows a random sender launching through the original launcher 0x3194e326 and through a per-launch copy by eth_call (new salt; only the exact replay reverts), and the engine trusts `to in LAUNCHERS` by address alone (code hashes are checked only for selector-found copies), so a 'follow' launch can be manufactured by anyone. Related: the 2e-6 ETH dust wallet 0xe71a69f4 (and snipers 0xb4d43149, 0x2be87ad7) buy in both operators' pools and thousands of others — it is a chain-wide dust bot, not a volume-bot fingerprint as HANDOFF 1 lists it; the handoff's own Oct 8 numbers need care: 10.1's '-$5.19' is really about -$8.5 (3 positive, not 4 wins), and new_scheme.py's docstring (42 launches, +8.9%) describes a run whose output was never committed (the file says 40, +9.0%).
- What is and is not reproducible in research/: the root research/ files are a fragment of an older project (reports 21.3/24.20/24.45/24.50, the 'Pons seat', paths src/analysis/ and data/derived/edge_check/Q/ that do not exist here); only v4_slowrug_test.py runs offline and reproduces slowrug_test.txt byte for byte (it writes a 763 KB slowrug_rows.json beside the input). Every session_oct9_10 script hardcodes /tmp/claude-0/-home-user-v2pons/f653fbde-aa08-514f-a7a0-470737d59aa4/scratchpad and /home/user/v2pons, and the key inputs were never copied (money/win.jsonl, ops/launcher_txs.json, ops/launcher_logs.json, origins/eras/p1.json, origins/vt.pkl, explore/grid.npy, pools.pkl, others/census.jsonl, all block caches), so eras/, fam/potpath, loop.py/signature.py and explore/sim.py cannot be re-run as copied, and several quoted paper results (6.12's +2.4% on 14 launches, the seat/newpaper/exits outputs) have no result file at all. The public RPC does answer from this container (front_audit/follow_audit were produced here on Oct 10 13:00-13:19) at roughly 45% HTTP 429.
- Operator A's money, beyond 'pot A about 7.01 ETH': the pot is pinned at ~7.0 ETH by design, grew to 8.6585 ETH in 37 minutes of bonding-curve launches on 0xe33e9e47 on Oct 8 (11:19-11:48) and was skimmed 1.6 ETH at 12:10 to hub 0x3ee51814, which bridged 0.8/1.0/0.25 ETH through Relay (receiver 0x4cd00e38, selector 0x49290c1c, rates 0.9955-0.9988) to Base addresses that all forward to the deposit sweeper 0x487cab40 -> hot wallet 0x3304e22d (29,656 ETH), the same terminal pot B uses; pot B (4.86 ETH, 31 wallets) ran the three lowest-fee launches on Oct 9 (170/220/912 ppm) and the Oct 8 copy scheme via collector 0x3fc5b8b8; the overnight launch wallets hold the pot (5.76-5.90 ETH) when the crowd sleeps; crowd A's trigger wallets 0x295877ce and 0x8700722d are late additions (nonces ~300-430) that any allowlist must include; and launch volumes (origins/eras/p4.out) fell from 200+ a day in August-September to 43 on Oct 9 and 5 on Oct 10, with none after 09:56 UTC Oct 10.
